# Plan: Move home_repeater from Stammdaten to BundMode (Issue #3)

## Context
Previous commit added `home_repeater` as an operator attribute in the Rufzeichen-Register (Stammdaten). User wants it instead as a per-exercise field in BundMode, next to the OP callsign input — same UX pattern. Store on `exercise_repeaters` table, not `operators`.

## Part 1: Revert previous commit's Stammdaten/Auswertung changes

Revert all 10 files touched by the previous commit. Specifically:

- **`server/src/routes/operators.ts`** — Remove `home_repeater` from POST destructuring/INSERT and PATCH fields array
- **`server/src/routes/exercises.ts`** — Remove `o.home_repeater` from stats participants query
- **`server/src/routes/reports.ts`** — Remove `o.home_repeater` from participants query
- **`server/src/routes/pdf.ts`** — Remove `o.home_repeater` from both participants queries
- **`server/src/services/pdf.ts`** — Remove `home_repeater` from Participant interface; revert renderParticipants (remove colRep, REP. header, home_repeater data cell, restore Name width to `colBz - colName - 5`)
- **`client/src/types/stats.ts`** — Remove `home_repeater` from Participant interface
- **`client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`** — Remove Repeater th/td from participants table
- **`client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`** — Remove Repeater th/td from participants table
- **`client/src/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx`** — Remove Repeater column from table header, display row, edit row, add form; reset `newOp` state

## Part 2: New implementation — home_repeater in BundMode

### 2a. Schema: `server/src/db/schema.ts`
- Add migration: `ALTER TABLE exercise_repeaters ADD COLUMN home_repeater TEXT DEFAULT NULL` with PRAGMA check
- Keep operators.home_repeater migration (already deployed, harmless unused column)

### 2b. API: `server/src/routes/exercises.ts`
- **POST** `/:id/repeaters` (line ~196): Accept `home_repeater` from body, add to INSERT
- **PATCH** `/:id/repeaters/:rid` (line ~210): Accept `home_repeater` from body, add to UPDATE

### 2c. Socket: `server/src/services/socket.ts`
- Add `home_repeater_updated` handler (line ~62, after `op_callsign_updated`), same pattern: validate UUID, broadcast to exercise room

### 2d. BundMode UI: `client/src/components/BundMode.tsx`
- **State** (line ~32): `const [blHomeRepeaters, setBlHomeRepeaters] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});`
- **Load** (line ~55 in useEffect): `homeReps[b.code] = exRep?.home_repeater || '';` then `setBlHomeRepeaters(homeReps);`
- **Save** function: `saveHomeRepeater(blCode, val)` — same POST-then-PATCH pattern as `saveOpCallsign`, emit `home_repeater_updated`
- **Socket listener** (line ~68 in useEffect): Listen for `home_repeater_updated`, map `repeater_id` → `blCode` via `blRepSelection`, update state
- **UI** (line ~284 area): Add after OP input:
  ```
  <label className="text-xs text-gray-500">REP:</label>
  <input ... value={blHomeRepeaters[bl.code]} onBlur={saveHomeRepeater} placeholder="Repeater" className="... w-20 sm:w-28" />
  ```

### 2e. CHANGELOG update

## Verification
1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run build"` compiles clean
2. BundMode: REP input visible next to OP, saves on blur, syncs via socket
3. OperatorsPage: No Repeater column (reverted)
4. Auswertung: No Repeater column (reverted)
5. Deploy with `./deploy-production.sh`

## Complete File Contents Report

### 1. **server/src/routes/operators.ts**
**Path:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/operators.ts`

This file exports an `operatorsRouter` Express router with CRUD endpoints for operators. Key features:
- `GET /` - Search/list operators with pagination
- `GET /:id` - Get single operator with history of exercises
- `POST /` - Create new operator with callsign validation, auto-derive Bundesland and Bezirk
- `PATCH /:id` - Update operator fields, supports auto-deriving bezirk from QTH when needed
- `DELETE /:id` - Delete operator (only if no signal reports reference them)

The router includes `home_repeater` field management and handles Bundesland/Bezirk assignment automatically.

---

### 2. **server/src/routes/exercises.ts**
**Path:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`

This file exports an `exercisesRouter` for managing exercises (crisis communication exercise sessions). Key endpoints:
- `GET /` - List all exercises with summary stats
- `POST /` - Create exercise (admin only), uses UUID for exercise IDs
- `GET /:id` - Get full exercise with repeaters, reports, attendance
- `PATCH /:id` - Update exercise metadata (admin only)
- `DELETE /:id` - Delete entire exercise (admin only)
- `GET /:id/stats` - Get comprehensive statistics including per-repeater, bezirk, Bundesland breakdowns
- `GET /:id/reports` - Get all signal reports for exercise
- `POST /:id/reports` - Create signal report with transaction for simultaneous attendance tracking
- `PATCH /:id/reports/:rid` - Update signal report
- `DELETE /:id/reports/:rid` - Delete signal report
- Repeaters management endpoints
- Attendance endpoints

---

### 3. **server/src/routes/reports.ts**
**Path:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/reports.ts`

This file exports a `reportsRouter` for aggregated reporting across date ranges. Key endpoints:
- `GET /stats` - Get aggregated statistics across multiple exercises in a date range, with optional exercise type filtering
- `GET /adif` - Generate ADIF export file (for QRZ.com, LOTW, etc.) for QSO records
- Supports complex SQL queries with dynamic placeholders for multiple exercise IDs

---

### 4. **server/src/routes/pdf.ts**
**Path:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/pdf.ts`

This file exports a `pdfRouter` for PDF generation. Key endpoints:
- `GET /reports` - Generate aggregated PDF report across date range with summary stats and charts
- `GET /exercises/:id` - Generate single-exercise PDF report with all stats and visualizations

Both endpoints gather comprehensive statistics and call PDF generation functions.

---

### 5. **server/src/services/pdf.ts**
**Path:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/services/pdf.ts`

This is a comprehensive PDF generation service using PDFKit library. Key features:
- Color constants and styling (Navy, Red, Gray palette)
- Austria SVG map data with Bundesland paths and label positioning
- Helper functions for heat maps, contrast-aware colors, and date formatting

  - Pie charts (report distribution)
  - Bezirk/Bundesland detail tables
  - Participant lists

- Landscape A4 layout with automatic page breaks

TypeScript interfaces for statistics data:

- `Participant` - Individual operator with callsign, location, report count, suffixes
- `RepeaterStat` - Report count per repeater
- `BlStat` - Bundesland (state) level statistics

### 10. **server/src/db/schema.ts**
**Path:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts`

Database schema definition and migrations. Key tables:
- `bundeslaender` - Austrian states with sort order and international flag
- `bezirke` - Districts within states, with capital flag
- `operators` - Ham radio callsigns with location, contact, equipment info, home repeater field
- `repeaters` - Repeater/transceiver stations with frequency, CTCSS, band, linked status, Bundesland, custom flag
- `einstiegspunkte` - Entry points for linked repeaters
- `exercises` - Crisis communication exercises with UUID IDs, metadata, type
- `exercise_repeaters` - Repeaters active in specific exercise with operator callsign
- `exercise_attendance` - Operator participation tracking
- `signal_reports` - QSO reports with RST, dB, bezirk tracking, suffix field
- `admins` - Admin user table with username/password_hash
- Multiple indexes for performance

Includes comprehensive migrations:
- Admins table restructuring (callsign→username/password)
- Exercise ID conversion (INTEGER→UUID)
- Column additions: suffix, bezirk_code, home_repeater
- Auto-assignment for single-bezirk states
- Backfill operations

---

### 11. **server/src/services/socket.ts**
**Path:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/services/socket.ts`

WebSocket service for real-time synchronization using Socket.IO. Features:
- JWT token authentication middleware
- UUID validation for exercise IDs
- Socket events:
  - `join_exercise` / `leave_exercise` - Join/leave exercise rooms
  - `report_created` / `report_updated` / `report_deleted` - Real-time report sync
  - `op_callsign_updated` - Update operator callsign for repeater
  - `attendance_updated` - Update attendance tracking
- Broadcasts events to other connected admins in same exercise room
- Attaches admin username to broadcasted data

---

### 12. **client/src/components/BundMode.tsx**
**Path:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/BundMode.tsx`

Complex component for Bundesland (BL) mode exercise entry. Features:
- Organizes data by Bundesland with collapsible sections
- Per-Bundesland repeater selection and operator callsign assignment
- Per-Bezirk (district) entry forms within each BL
- Creates and manages custom repeaters per Bundesland
- Supports callsign suffixes (/m, /p, /am, /mm) with toggle buttons
- Drag-and-drop to move reports between bezirks
- Inline editing of existing reports
- Automatic operator name/QTH saving
- Real-time socket updates for operator callsigns
- Parses RST format (readability/strength with optional dB modifier)
- Handles report duplication (updates instead of creates)
- Responsive form layout for name, QTH, repeater, rapport, notes fields

---

## Summary

This is a comprehensive ham radio crisis communication exercise logging system with:
- **Backend:** Express.js with SQLite database, Socket.IO real-time sync
- **Frontend:** React 18 with TypeScript, Chart.js visualizations, PDF generation
- **Key features:** Multi-Bundesland organization, repeater management, comprehensive reporting, PDF/ADIF exports, real-time collaboration, drag-and-drop UI, responsive design

     6→export const operatorsRouter = Router();

     9→operatorsRouter.get('/', (req, res) => {
    10→  const db = getDb();
    11→  const q = req.query.q as string;
    12→  if (q) {
    13→    const pattern = `%${q.toUpperCase()}%`;
    14→    const operators = db.prepare(`
    15→      SELECT o.*, bl.name as bundesland_name,
    16→        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports sr WHERE sr.operator_id = o.id AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL) as total_reports
    17→      FROM operators o
    18→      LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    19→      WHERE o.callsign LIKE ? OR UPPER(o.name) LIKE ? OR UPPER(o.qth) LIKE ?
    20→      ORDER BY o.callsign LIMIT 50
    21→    `).all(pattern, pattern, pattern);
    22→    res.json(operators);
    23→  } else {
    24→    const limit = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(req.query.limit as string) || 100, 1), 1000);
    25→    const offset = Math.max(parseInt(req.query.offset as string) || 0, 0);
    26→    const operators = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators ORDER BY callsign LIMIT ? OFFSET ?').all(limit, offset);
    27→    const total = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM operators').get() as any).c;
    28→    res.json({ operators, total });
    29→  }

    36→  if (!operator) {

    44→        ELSE COALESCE(sr.readability || '/' || sr.strength || COALESCE(sr.db_over_s9, ''), '—')
    45→        END, ' | ') as reports

    47→    JOIN exercises e ON e.id = sr.exercise_id
    48→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    49→    WHERE sr.operator_id = ?
    50→    GROUP BY e.id
    51→    ORDER BY e.date DESC

   102→  const db = getDb();

   104→  const updates: string[] = [];
   105→  const params: any[] = [];
   106→  for (const f of fields) {
   107→    if (req.body[f] !== undefined) {
   108→      updates.push(`${f} = ?`);
   109→      params.push(f === 'callsign' ? req.body[f].toUpperCase() : req.body[f]);
   110→    }
   111→  }
   112→  // Auto-derive bezirk from QTH when QTH changes and bezirk_code not explicitly set
   113→  if (req.body.qth !== undefined && req.body.bezirk_code === undefined) {
   114→    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT bundesland_code, bezirk_code FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id) as any;
   115→    if (existing && !existing.bezirk_code) {

   117→      const derived = qthToBezirkCode(db, req.body.qth, bl);
   118→      if (derived) {
   119→        updates.push('bezirk_code = ?');
   120→        params.push(derived);
   121→      }
   122→    }
   123→  }
   124→  if (updates.length === 0) {
   125→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Keine Änderungen' });
   126→    return;
   127→  }
   128→  updates.push("updated_at = datetime('now')");
   129→  params.push(req.params.id);
   130→  db.prepare(`UPDATE operators SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
   131→  const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
   132→  res.json(operator);

    57→      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
    58→    FROM signal_reports sr
    59→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    60→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    61→    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
    62→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ?
    63→    ORDER BY r.bundesland_code, o.callsign
    64→  `).all(req.params.id);
    65→
    66→  const attendance = db.prepare(`
    67→    SELECT ea.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code
    68→    FROM exercise_attendance ea
    69→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = ea.operator_id
    70→    WHERE ea.exercise_id = ?
    71→    ORDER BY o.callsign
    72→  `).all(req.params.id);
    73→
    74→  res.json({ ...exercise, repeaters, reports, attendance });
    75→});
    76→
    77→// Update exercise notes/name (admin only)
    78→exercisesRouter.patch('/:id', requireRole('admin'), (req, res) => {
    79→  const db = getDb();
    80→  const { notes, name, oe_link_enabled, organisator, exercise_type, simplex_bezirke, simplex_bl_codes } = req.body;
    81→  const updates: string[] = [];
    82→  const params: any[] = [];
    83→  if (notes !== undefined) { updates.push('notes = ?'); params.push(notes); }
    84→  if (name !== undefined) { updates.push('name = ?'); params.push(name); }
    85→  if (oe_link_enabled !== undefined) { updates.push('oe_link_enabled = ?'); params.push(oe_link_enabled ? 1 : 0); }
    86→  if (organisator !== undefined) { updates.push('organisator = ?'); params.push(organisator || null); }
    87→  if (exercise_type !== undefined) { updates.push('exercise_type = ?'); params.push(exercise_type); }
    88→  if (simplex_bezirke !== undefined) { updates.push('simplex_bezirke = ?'); params.push(simplex_bezirke ? 1 : 0); }
    89→  if (simplex_bl_codes !== undefined) { updates.push('simplex_bl_codes = ?'); params.push(simplex_bl_codes || null); }
    90→  if (updates.length === 0) {
    91→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Keine Änderungen' });
    92→    return;
    93→  }
    94→  params.push(req.params.id);
    95→  db.prepare(`UPDATE exercises SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
    96→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    97→  res.json(exercise);
    98→});
    99→
   100→// Delete exercise (admin only)
   101→exercisesRouter.delete('/:id', requireRole('admin'), (req, res) => {
   102→  const db = getDb();
   103→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT id, name, date FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
   104→  if (!exercise) {
   105→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });
   106→    return;
   107→  }
   108→  const deleteAll = db.transaction(() => {
   109→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   110→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercise_attendance WHERE exercise_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   111→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercise_repeaters WHERE exercise_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   112→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   113→  });
   114→  deleteAll();
   115→  res.json({ success: true });
   116→});
   117→
   118→// Get exercise stats
   119→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/stats', (req, res) => {
   120→  const db = getDb();
   121→  const eid = req.params.id;
   122→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   123→  const totalReports = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   124→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(`
   125→    SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
   126→    FROM signal_reports sr
   127→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   128→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
   129→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
   130→    ORDER BY r.sort_order
   131→  `).all(eid);
   132→
   133→  // Bezirk breakdown: participants + reports per bezirk, grouped by bundesland
   134→  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(`
   135→    SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
   136→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
   137→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   138→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   139→    FROM signal_reports sr
   140→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
   141→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
   142→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   143→    GROUP BY bz.code
   144→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code
   145→  `).all(eid);
   146→
   147→  // Bundesland breakdown
   148→  const blStats = db.prepare(`
   149→    SELECT
   150→      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
   151→      o.bundesland_code,
   152→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   153→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   154→    FROM signal_reports sr
   155→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   156→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   157→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   158→    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
   159→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code
   160→  `).all(eid);
   161→
   162→  // Participant list: all operators with their report counts
   163→  const participants = db.prepare(`
   164→    SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code, o.home_repeater,
   165→      bl.name as bundesland_name,
   166→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count,
   167→      GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT sr.suffix) as suffixes
   168→    FROM signal_reports sr
   169→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   170→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   171→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   172→    GROUP BY sr.operator_id
   173→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign
   174→  `).all(eid);
   175→
   176→  res.json({ totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants });
   177→});
   178→
   179→// Exercise repeaters management
   180→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/repeaters', (req, res) => {
   181→  const db = getDb();
   182→  const repeaters = db.prepare(`
   183→    SELECT er.*, r.short_name, r.site_name, r.band, r.callsign as repeater_callsign,
   184→      r.frequency_mhz, r.offset_mhz, r.ctcss_hz, r.burst_hz, r.type, r.is_linked, r.sort_order,
   185→      r.bundesland_code, bl.name as bundesland_name

   225→// Signal Reports CRUD
   226→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/reports', (req, res) => {
   227→  const db = getDb();
   228→  const reports = db.prepare(`
   229→    SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.qth as operator_qth, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
   230→      r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,
   231→      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name

   235→    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id

   242→exercisesRouter.post('/:id/reports', (req, res) => {
   243→  const db = getDb();
   244→  const admin = (req as AuthRequest).admin!;
   245→  const { operator_id, repeater_id, readability, strength, db_over_s9, einstiegspunkt_id, is_op_marker, notes, bezirk_code, suffix } = req.body;
   246→  console.log(`[report] POST exercise=${req.params.id} op=${operator_id} rep=${repeater_id} r=${readability} s=${strength} bz=${bezirk_code || '?'} suffix=${suffix || ''} by=${admin.username}`);
   247→
   248→  if (!operator_id || !repeater_id) {
   249→    console.log('[report] REJECTED: missing operator_id or repeater_id');
   250→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'operator_id und repeater_id erforderlich' });
   251→    return;
   252→  }
   253→
   254→  try {
   255→    const insertReportAndAttendance = db.transaction(() => {
   256→      const result = db.prepare(`
   257→        INSERT INTO signal_reports (exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id, readability, strength, db_over_s9, einstiegspunkt_id, is_op_marker, notes, entered_by, bezirk_code, suffix)
   258→        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
   259→      `).run(req.params.id, operator_id, repeater_id, readability || null, strength || null, db_over_s9 || null, einstiegspunkt_id || null, is_op_marker ? 1 : 0, notes || null, admin.username, bezirk_code || null, suffix || null);
   260→
   261→      db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO exercise_attendance (exercise_id, operator_id, entered_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
   262→        .run(req.params.id, operator_id, admin.username);
   263→
   264→      return result;
   265→    });
   266→
   267→    const result = insertReportAndAttendance();
   268→
   269→    const report = db.prepare(`
   270→      SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
   271→        r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,
   272→        ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
   273→      FROM signal_reports sr
   274→      JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   275→      JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   276→      LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
   277→      WHERE sr.id = ?
   278→    `).get(result.lastInsertRowid);
   279→
   280→    res.status(201).json(report);
   281→  } catch (e: any) {
   282→    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE')) {
   283→      const existing = db.prepare(`
   284→        SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
   285→          r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,
   286→          ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
   287→        FROM signal_reports sr
   288→        JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   289→        JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   290→        LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
   291→        WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.operator_id = ? AND sr.repeater_id = ?
   292→      `).get(req.params.id, operator_id, repeater_id);
   293→      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Rapport für diesen Operator auf diesem Umsetzer existiert bereits', existing_report: existing });
   294→    } else {
   295→      throw e;
   296→    }
   297→  }
   298→});
   299→
   300→exercisesRouter.patch('/:id/reports/:rid', (req, res) => {
   301→  const db = getDb();

   303→  const updates: string[] = [];

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→
     4→export const reportsRouter = Router();
     5→
     6→// Aggregated stats across a date range
     7→reportsRouter.get('/stats', (req, res) => {
     8→  const { from, to, types } = req.query;
     9→  if (!from || !to) {
    10→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
    11→    return;
    12→  }
    13→
    14→  const db = getDb();
    15→
    16→  // Fetch exercises in date range, optionally filtered by type
    17→  let typeFilter = '';
    18→  const queryParams: any[] = [from, to];
    19→  if (types && typeof types === 'string' && types.trim()) {
    20→    const typeList = types.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    21→    if (typeList.length > 0) {
    22→      typeFilter = ` AND e.exercise_type IN (${typeList.map(() => '?').join(',')})`;
    23→      queryParams.push(...typeList);
    24→    }
    25→  }
    26→
    27→  const exercises = db.prepare(
    28→    `SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.oe_link_enabled, e.exercise_type,
    29→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
    30→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
    31→    FROM exercises e
    32→    WHERE e.date >= ? AND e.date <= ?${typeFilter}
    33→    ORDER BY e.date ASC`
    34→  ).all(...queryParams) as any[];
    35→
    36→  if (exercises.length === 0) {
    37→    res.json({
    38→      exercises: [],
    39→      stats: { totalParticipants: 0, totalReports: 0, perRepeater: [], bezirkStats: [], blStats: [], participants: [] },
    40→    });
    41→    return;
    42→  }
    43→
    44→  const ids = exercises.map((e: any) => e.id);
    45→  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');
    46→
    47→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare(
    48→    `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0`
    49→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    50→
    51→  const totalReports = (db.prepare(
    52→    `SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL`
    53→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    54→
    55→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(
    56→    `SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
    57→    FROM signal_reports sr
    58→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    59→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
    60→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
    61→    ORDER BY count DESC`
    62→  ).all(...ids);
    63→
    64→  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(
    65→    `SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
    66→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
    67→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    68→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    69→    FROM signal_reports sr
    70→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
    71→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
    72→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    73→    GROUP BY bz.code
    74→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code`
    75→  ).all(...ids);
    76→
    77→  const blStats = db.prepare(
    78→    `SELECT
    79→      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
    80→      o.bundesland_code,
    81→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    82→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    83→    FROM signal_reports sr
    84→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    85→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    86→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    87→    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
    88→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code`
    89→  ).all(...ids);
    90→
    91→  const participants = db.prepare(
    92→    `SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code, o.home_repeater,
    93→      bl.name as bundesland_name,
    94→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count,
    95→      GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT sr.suffix) as suffixes
    96→    FROM signal_reports sr
    97→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    98→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    99→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   100→    GROUP BY sr.operator_id
   101→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign`
   102→  ).all(...ids);
   103→
   104→  res.json({
   105→    exercises,
   106→    stats: { totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants },
   107→  });
   108→});
   109→
   110→// ADIF export across a date range
   111→reportsRouter.get('/adif', (req, res) => {
   112→  const { from, to } = req.query;
   113→  if (!from || !to) {
   114→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
   115→    return;
   116→  }
   117→
   118→  const db = getDb();
   119→
   120→  const exercises = db.prepare(
   121→    `SELECT id, date FROM exercises WHERE date >= ? AND date <= ? ORDER BY date ASC`
   122→  ).all(from, to) as { id: string; date: string }[];
   123→
   124→  if (exercises.length === 0) {
   125→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Keine Übungen im gewählten Zeitraum' });
   126→    return;
   127→  }
   128→
   129→  const ids = exercises.map(e => e.id);
   130→  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');
   131→
   132→  const reports = db.prepare(
   133→    `SELECT sr.readability, sr.strength, sr.db_over_s9, sr.notes, sr.created_at,
   134→      o.callsign, o.name as op_name, o.qth,
   135→      r.short_name, r.frequency_mhz, r.band, r.type,
   136→      e.date as exercise_date
   137→    FROM signal_reports sr
   138→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   139→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   140→    JOIN exercises e ON e.id = sr.exercise_id
   141→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders})
   142→      AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   143→      AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
   144→    ORDER BY e.date, sr.created_at`
   145→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
   146→
   147→  // Build ADIF
   148→  const adifField = (name: string, value: string) => {
   149→    if (!value) return '';
   150→    return `<${name}:${value.length}>${value} `;
   151→  };
   152→
   153→  let adif = '';
   154→
   155→  // Header
   156→  adif += 'ADIF Export from BOS-ARSA Log\n';
   157→  adif += `https://bosarsalog.oeradio.at/\n`;
   158→  adif += `Date range: ${from} to ${to}\n`;
   159→  adif += `QSO count: ${reports.length}\n`;
   160→  adif += '\n';
   161→  adif += adifField('ADIF_VER', '3.1.4');
   162→  adif += adifField('PROGRAMID', 'BOS-ARSA Log');
   163→  adif += adifField('PROGRAMVERSION', '1.0.5');
   164→  adif += '\n<EOH>\n\n';
   165→
   166→  // Records
   167→  for (const r of reports) {
   168→    const qsoDate = r.exercise_date.replace(/-/g, '');
   169→
   170→    // Extract time from created_at (format: "2026-02-16 14:30:00")
   171→    let timeOn = '0900'; // default
   172→    if (r.created_at) {
   173→      const timePart = r.created_at.split(' ')[1];
   174→      if (timePart) {
   175→        timeOn = timePart.replace(/:/g, '').slice(0, 4);
   176→      }
   177→    }
   178→
   179→    // RST: combine readability + strength + optional dB
   180→    const rst = `${r.readability}${r.strength}`;
   181→
   182→    // Frequency in MHz as string
   183→    const freq = r.frequency_mhz ? String(r.frequency_mhz) : '';
   184→
   185→    // Band mapping
   186→    let band = r.band || '';
   187→    if (!band && r.frequency_mhz) {
   188→      if (r.frequency_mhz >= 144 && r.frequency_mhz < 148) band = '2m';
   189→      else if (r.frequency_mhz >= 430 && r.frequency_mhz < 440) band = '70cm';
   190→      else if (r.frequency_mhz >= 1240 && r.frequency_mhz < 1300) band = '23cm';
   191→    }
   192→
   193→    adif += adifField('CALL', r.callsign);
   194→    adif += adifField('QSO_DATE', qsoDate);
   195→    adif += adifField('TIME_ON', timeOn);
   196→    adif += adifField('MODE', 'FM');
   197→    if (band) adif += adifField('BAND', band);
   198→    if (freq) adif += adifField('FREQ', freq);
   199→    adif += adifField('RST_SENT', rst);
   200→    adif += adifField('RST_RCVD', rst);
   201→    if (r.op_name) adif += adifField('NAME', r.op_name);
   202→    if (r.qth) adif += adifField('QTH', r.qth);
   203→    if (r.notes) adif += adifField('COMMENT', r.notes);
   204→    adif += '<EOR>\n';
   205→  }
   206→
   207→  const filename = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   208→  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8');
   209→  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="${filename}"`);
   210→  res.send(adif);
   211→});
   212→
   213→// Placeholder fallback
   214→reportsRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {
   215→  res.json({ message: 'Use /api/v1/reports/stats?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD' });
   216→});
   217→

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→import { generateAggregatedPdf, generateExercisePdf } from '../services/pdf.js';
     4→
     5→export const pdfRouter = Router();
     6→
     7→// GET /api/v1/pdf/reports?from=...&to=...&types=...
     8→// Aggregated PDF across a date range
     9→pdfRouter.get('/reports', (req, res) => {
    10→  const { from, to, types } = req.query;
    11→  if (!from || !to) {
    12→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
    13→    return;
    14→  }
    15→
    16→  const db = getDb();
    17→
    18→  let typeFilter = '';
    19→  const queryParams: any[] = [from, to];
    20→  if (types && typeof types === 'string' && types.trim()) {
    21→    const typeList = types.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    22→    if (typeList.length > 0) {
    23→      typeFilter = ` AND e.exercise_type IN (${typeList.map(() => '?').join(',')})`;
    24→      queryParams.push(...typeList);
    25→    }
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  const exercises = db.prepare(
    29→    `SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.exercise_type,
    30→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
    31→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
    32→    FROM exercises e
    33→    WHERE e.date >= ? AND e.date <= ?${typeFilter}
    34→    ORDER BY e.date ASC`
    35→  ).all(...queryParams) as any[];
    36→
    37→  if (exercises.length === 0) {
    38→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Keine Übungen im gewählten Zeitraum' });
    39→    return;
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  const ids = exercises.map((e: any) => e.id);
    43→  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');
    44→
    45→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare(
    46→    `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0`
    47→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    48→
    49→  const totalReports = (db.prepare(
    50→    `SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL`
    51→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    52→
    53→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(
    54→    `SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
    55→    FROM signal_reports sr
    56→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    57→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
    58→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
    59→    ORDER BY count DESC`
    60→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
    61→
    62→  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(
    63→    `SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
    64→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
    65→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    66→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    67→    FROM signal_reports sr
    68→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
    69→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
    70→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    71→    GROUP BY bz.code
    72→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code`
    73→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
    74→
    75→  const blStats = db.prepare(
    76→    `SELECT
    77→      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
    78→      o.bundesland_code,
    79→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    80→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    81→    FROM signal_reports sr
    82→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    83→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    84→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    85→    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
    86→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code`
    87→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
    88→
    89→  const participants = db.prepare(
    90→    `SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code, o.home_repeater,
    91→      bl.name as bundesland_name,
    92→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count,
    93→      GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT sr.suffix) as suffixes

    95→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    96→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    97→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    98→    GROUP BY sr.operator_id
    99→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign`
   100→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
   101→
   102→  const stats = { totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants };
   103→
   104→  const pdfDoc = generateAggregatedPdf(exercises, stats, from as string, to as string);
   105→
   106→  const filename = `BOS-ARSA_Auswertung_${from}_${to}.pdf`;
   107→  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
   108→  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="${filename}"`);
   109→  pdfDoc.pipe(res);

    87→
    88→const BL_SHORT: Record<string, string> = {
    89→  '01': 'OE1', '02': 'OE2', '03': 'OE3', '04': 'OE4', '05': 'OE5',
    90→  '06': 'OE6', '07': 'OE7', '08': 'OE8', '09': 'OE9',
    91→};
    92→
    93→// ── Helper functions ─────────────────────────────────────
    94→
    95→function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    96→  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';
    97→  const t = value / max;
    98→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    99→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
   100→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
   101→  return '#' + [r, g, b].map(c => c.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
   102→}
   103→
   104→/** Return white for dark backgrounds, navy for light backgrounds */
   105→function getLabelColor(hexBg: string): string {
   106→  const r = parseInt(hexBg.slice(1, 3), 16);
   107→  const g = parseInt(hexBg.slice(3, 5), 16);
   108→  const b = parseInt(hexBg.slice(5, 7), 16);
   109→  // Relative luminance (perceived brightness)
   110→  const lum = (0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b);
   111→  return lum < 150 ? WHITE : NAVY;
   112→}
   113→
   114→function formatDateDE(d: string): string {
   115→  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
   116→  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
   117→}
   118→
   119→function ensureSpace(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, needed: number) {
   120→  const bottom = doc.page.height - doc.page.margins.bottom;
   121→  if (doc.y + needed > bottom) {
   122→    doc.addPage();
   123→  }
   124→}
   125→
   126→/** Write text at fixed position without moving doc.y */
   127→function textAt(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, str: string, x: number, y: number, opts?: PDFKit.Mixins.TextOptions) {
   128→  doc.text(str, x, y, { lineBreak: false, ...opts });
   129→}
   130→
   131→// ── Render functions ─────────────────────────────────────
   132→
   133→function renderHeader(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, title: string, subtitle: string) {
   134→  const pageW = doc.page.width;
   135→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   136→  const barW = pageW - m.left - m.right;
   137→
   138→  doc.save();
   139→  doc.rect(m.left, m.top, barW, 50).fill(NAVY);
   140→  doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(WHITE);
   141→  textAt(doc, 'BOS-ARSA Log', m.left + 15, m.top + 10);
   142→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor('#93c5fd');
   143→  textAt(doc, 'Im Sinne der Sicherheit', m.left + 15, m.top + 32);
   144→  doc.restore();
   145→
   146→  doc.y = m.top + 60;
   147→  doc.fontSize(14).fillColor(NAVY);
   148→  textAt(doc, title, m.left, doc.y);
   149→  doc.y += 18;
   150→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   151→  textAt(doc, subtitle, m.left, doc.y);
   152→  doc.y += 16;
   153→}
   154→
   155→function renderSummaryCards(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, stats: PdfStats, exerciseCount?: number) {
   156→  ensureSpace(doc, 50);
   157→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   158→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   159→  const cols = exerciseCount !== undefined ? 4 : 2;
   160→  const cardW = usable / cols - 8;
   161→  const startY = doc.y;
   162→
   163→  const cards: { value: string | number; label: string }[] = [
   164→    { value: stats.totalParticipants, label: 'Teilnehmer' },
   165→    { value: stats.totalReports, label: 'Rapporte' },
   166→  ];
   167→  if (exerciseCount !== undefined) {
   168→    cards.push({ value: exerciseCount, label: 'Übungen' });
   169→    const avg = exerciseCount > 0 ? Math.round(stats.totalParticipants / exerciseCount * 10) / 10 : 0;
   170→    cards.push({ value: avg, label: 'Ø Teiln./Übung' });
   171→  }
   172→
   173→  cards.forEach((c, i) => {
   174→    const x = m.left + i * (cardW + 8);
   175→    doc.save();
   176→    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 40, 4).fill(GRAY_50);
   177→    doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(NAVY);
   178→    textAt(doc, String(c.value), x, startY + 5, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   179→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   180→    textAt(doc, c.label, x, startY + 27, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   181→    doc.restore();
   182→  });
   183→  doc.y = startY + 48;
   184→}
   185→
   186→function renderPerRepeater(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, perRepeater: PerRepeater[]) {
   187→  if (perRepeater.length === 0) return;
   188→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   189→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   190→  const gap = 6;
   191→  const maxCols = 6;
   192→  const cols = Math.min(perRepeater.length, maxCols);
   193→  const cardW = usable / cols - gap;
   194→  const rowH = 28;
   195→  const rows = Math.ceil(perRepeater.length / maxCols);
   196→  ensureSpace(doc, rows * (rowH + gap) + 4);
   197→
   198→  const startY = doc.y;
   199→  perRepeater.forEach((r, i) => {
   200→    const col = i % maxCols;
   201→    const row = Math.floor(i / maxCols);
   202→    const x = m.left + col * (cardW + gap);
   203→    const y = startY + row * (rowH + gap);
   204→    doc.save();
   205→    doc.roundedRect(x, y, cardW, rowH, 3).fill(GRAY_50);
   206→    doc.fontSize(12).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   207→    textAt(doc, String(r.count), x, y + 2, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   208→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   209→    textAt(doc, r.short_name, x, y + 17, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   210→    doc.restore();
   211→  });
   212→  doc.y = startY + rows * (rowH + gap) + 4;
   213→}
   214→
   215→function renderExerciseTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, exercises: ExerciseSummary[]) {
   216→  if (exercises.length === 0) return;
   217→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   218→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   219→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   220→
   221→  // Section header
   222→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   223→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   224→  textAt(doc, `Übungen (${exercises.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   225→  doc.y += 20;
   226→
   227→  // Column positions
   228→  const colDate = m.left + 5;
   229→  const colName = m.left + 80;
   230→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   231→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   232→
   233→  // Column headers
   234→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   235→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   236→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   237→  textAt(doc, 'DATUM', colDate, hy);
   238→  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);
   239→  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   240→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   241→  doc.y += 16;
   242→
   243→  exercises.forEach((ex, i) => {
   244→    ensureSpace(doc, 14);
   245→    const rowY = doc.y;
   246→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   247→      doc.save();
   248→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);
   249→      doc.restore();
   250→    }
   251→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   252→    textAt(doc, formatDateDE(ex.date), colDate, rowY + 2);
   253→    textAt(doc, ex.name || '', colName, rowY + 2, { width: colPart - colName - 10 });
   254→    textAt(doc, String(ex.participant_count), colPart, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   255→    textAt(doc, String(ex.report_count), colRap, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   256→    doc.y = rowY + 13;
   257→  });
   258→  doc.y += 4;
   259→}
   260→
   261→function renderAustriaMap(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[]) {
   262→  if (blStats.length === 0) return;
   263→  ensureSpace(doc, 200);
   264→
   265→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   266→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   267→  const mapW = Math.min(usable, 400);
   268→  const mapH = mapW * 0.5;
   269→  const offsetX = m.left + (usable - mapW) / 2;
   270→
   271→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   272→  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland', m.left, doc.y);
   273→  doc.y += 14;
   274→  const mapStartY = doc.y;
   275→
   276→  const dataMap = new Map(blStats.map(bl => [bl.bundesland_code, bl]));
   277→  const maxP = Math.max(...blStats.map(bl => bl.participants), 1);
   278→
   279→  const svgW = 620;
   280→  const svgH = 300;
   281→  const scale = Math.min(mapW / svgW, mapH / svgH);
   282→
   283→  for (const [code, pathData] of Object.entries(BL_PATHS)) {
   284→    const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   285→    const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   286→    const fillColor = getHeatColor(participants, maxP);
   287→
   288→    doc.save();
   289→    doc.translate(offsetX, mapStartY);
   290→    doc.scale(scale);
   291→    doc.path(pathData.d).lineWidth(2 / scale).fillAndStroke(fillColor, WHITE);
   292→    doc.restore();
   293→
   294→    // Label — contrast-aware color based on heatmap background
   295→    const lx = offsetX + pathData.labelX * scale;
   296→    const ly = mapStartY + pathData.labelY * scale;
   297→    const labelColor = getLabelColor(fillColor);
   298→    doc.save();
   299→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(labelColor);
   300→    textAt(doc, BL_SHORT[code] || code, lx - 15, ly - 6, { width: 30, align: 'center' });
   301→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(labelColor);
   302→    textAt(doc, String(participants), lx - 15, ly + 1, { width: 30, align: 'center' });
   303→    doc.restore();
   304→  }
   305→
   306→  doc.y = mapStartY + mapH + 10;
   307→}
   308→
   309→function renderBarChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; participants: number; reports: number }[]) {
   310→  if (chartData.length === 0) return;
   311→  ensureSpace(doc, 180);
   312→
   313→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   314→  const chartW = Math.min(doc.page.width - m.left - m.right, 500);
   315→  const chartH = 140;
   316→  const startX = m.left;
   317→
   318→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   319→  textAt(doc, 'Stationen & Rapporte nach Bundesland', m.left, doc.y);
   320→  doc.y += 15;
   321→  const startY = doc.y;
   322→
   323→  const maxVal = Math.max(...chartData.map(d => Math.max(d.participants, d.reports)), 1);
   324→  const barAreaW = chartW - 30;
   325→  const groupW = barAreaW / chartData.length;
   326→  const barW = Math.min(groupW * 0.35, 20);
   327→
   328→  // Y-axis gridlines
   329→  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
   330→    const y = startY + chartH - (chartH * i / 4);
   331→    const val = Math.round(maxVal * i / 4);
   332→    doc.save();
   333→    doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(startX + 25, y).lineTo(startX + chartW, y).stroke();
   334→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   335→    textAt(doc, String(val), startX, y - 3, { width: 22, align: 'right' });
   336→    doc.restore();
   337→  }
   338→
   339→  // Bars
   340→  chartData.forEach((d, i) => {
   341→    const groupX = startX + 30 + i * groupW;
   342→    const pH = (d.participants / maxVal) * chartH;
   343→    doc.rect(groupX, startY + chartH - pH, barW, pH).fill(NAVY);
   344→    const rH = (d.reports / maxVal) * chartH;
   345→    doc.rect(groupX + barW + 2, startY + chartH - rH, barW, rH).fill(RED);
   346→    doc.save();
   347→    doc.fontSize(4).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   348→    textAt(doc, d.label, groupX - 2, startY + chartH + 3, { width: groupW });
   349→    doc.restore();
   350→  });
   351→
   352→  // Legend
   353→  const legendY = startY + chartH + 14;
   354→  doc.rect(startX + 30, legendY, 8, 6).fill(NAVY);
   355→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   356→  textAt(doc, 'Stationen', startX + 42, legendY);
   357→  doc.rect(startX + 90, legendY, 8, 6).fill(RED);
   358→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   359→  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte', startX + 102, legendY);
   360→  doc.y = legendY + 14;
   361→}
   362→
   363→function renderPieChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; value: number }[]) {
   364→  const filtered = chartData.filter(d => d.value > 0);
   365→  if (filtered.length === 0) return;
   366→  ensureSpace(doc, 180);
   367→
   368→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   369→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   370→
   371→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   372→  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte Verteilung', m.left, doc.y);
   373→  doc.y += 12;
   374→
   375→  const cx = m.left + 100;
   376→  const cy = doc.y + 70;
   377→  const r = 60;
   378→  const total = filtered.reduce((s, d) => s + d.value, 0);
   379→  let startAngle = -Math.PI / 2;
   380→
   381→  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {
   382→    const sliceAngle = (d.value / total) * 2 * Math.PI;
   383→    const endAngle = startAngle + sliceAngle;
   384→    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];
   385→
   386→    const x1 = cx + r * Math.cos(startAngle);
   387→    const y1 = cy + r * Math.sin(startAngle);
   388→    const x2 = cx + r * Math.cos(endAngle);
   389→    const y2 = cy + r * Math.sin(endAngle);
   390→    const largeArc = sliceAngle > Math.PI ? 1 : 0;
   391→
   392→    doc.path(`M ${cx} ${cy} L ${x1} ${y1} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${largeArc} 1 ${x2} ${y2} Z`).fill(color);
   393→    startAngle = endAngle;
   394→  });
   395→
   396→  // Legend (limit to available space)
   397→  const legendX = cx + r + 30;
   398→  const maxLegendItems = Math.floor(((doc.page.height - m.bottom) - (cy - r)) / 12);
   399→  const legendItems = filtered.slice(0, maxLegendItems);
   400→  legendItems.forEach((d, i) => {
   401→    const ly = cy - r + i * 12;
   402→    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];
   403→    doc.rect(legendX, ly, 8, 8).fill(color);
   404→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   405→    textAt(doc, `${d.label} (${d.value})`, legendX + 12, ly + 1, { width: usable - legendX + m.left - 12 });
   406→  });
   407→  if (filtered.length > maxLegendItems) {
   408→    const overflowY = cy - r + maxLegendItems * 12;
   409→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   410→    textAt(doc, `… +${filtered.length - maxLegendItems} weitere`, legendX, overflowY);
   411→  }
   412→
   413→  doc.y = cy + r + 15;
   414→}
   415→
   416→function renderBezirkTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, bezirkStats: BezirkStat[], stats: PdfStats) {
   417→  if (bezirkStats.length === 0) return;
   418→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   419→
   420→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   421→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   422→  const colName = m.left + 5;
   423→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   424→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   425→
   426→  // Section header
   427→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   428→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   429→  textAt(doc, 'Nebenstationen nach Bezirk', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   430→  doc.y += 20;
   431→
   432→  // Column headers
   433→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   434→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   435→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   436→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND / BEZIRK', colName, hy);
   437→  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   438→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   439→  doc.y += 16;
   440→
   441→  // Group by Bundesland
   442→  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalP: number; totalR: number }> = {};
   443→  for (const bz of bezirkStats) {
   444→    if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {
   445→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalP: 0, totalR: 0 };
   446→    }
   447→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);
   448→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalP += bz.participants;
   449→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalR += bz.reports;
   450→  }
   451→
   452→  for (const [, bl] of Object.entries(byBl)) {
   453→    ensureSpace(doc, 14);
   454→    const rowY = doc.y;
   455→    doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 13).fill(GRAY_100);
   456→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   457→    textAt(doc, bl.name, colName, rowY + 3);
   458→    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalP), colPart, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   459→    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalR), colRap, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   460→    doc.y = rowY + 14;
   461→
   462→    bl.bezirke.forEach(bz => {
   463→      ensureSpace(doc, 12);
   464→      const ry = doc.y;
   465→      doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);
   466→      textAt(doc, `    ${bz.bezirk_code}  ${bz.bezirk_name}`, colName, ry + 1);
   467→      textAt(doc, String(bz.participants), colPart, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   468→      textAt(doc, String(bz.reports), colRap, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   469→      doc.y = ry + 11;
   470→    });
   471→  }
   472→
   473→  // Gesamt
   474→  ensureSpace(doc, 18);
   475→  const gy = doc.y;
   476→  doc.rect(m.left, gy, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);
   477→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE);
   478→  textAt(doc, 'Gesamt', colName, gy + 4);
   479→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   480→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   481→  doc.y = gy + 20;
   482→}
   483→
   484→function renderBlTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[], stats: PdfStats) {
   485→  if (blStats.length === 0) return;
   486→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);

   492→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   493→
   494→  // Section header
   495→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   496→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   497→  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland / Land', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   498→  doc.y += 20;
   499→
   500→  // Column headers

   505→  textAt(doc, 'TEILNEHMER', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   506→  textAt(doc, 'RAPPORTE', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });

   509→  blStats.forEach((bl, i) => {

   524→  // Gesamt

   531→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   532→  doc.y = gy + 20;

   534→
   535→function renderParticipants(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, participants: Participant[]) {
   536→  if (participants.length === 0) return;
   537→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   538→
   539→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   540→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   541→
   542→  // Section header
   543→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   544→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   545→  textAt(doc, `Teilnehmer-Liste (${participants.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   546→  doc.y += 20;
   547→
   548→  // Column positions
   549→  const colNum = m.left + 3;
   550→  const colCall = m.left + 25;
   551→  const colName = m.left + 90;
   552→  const colRep = m.left + usable - 185;
   553→  const colBz = m.left + usable - 135;
   554→  const colBl = m.left + usable - 90;
   555→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 35;
   556→
   557→  // Column headers
   558→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   559→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   560→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   561→  textAt(doc, '#', colNum, hy);
   562→  textAt(doc, 'RUFZEICHEN', colCall, hy);
   563→  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);
   564→  textAt(doc, 'REP.', colRep, hy);
   565→  textAt(doc, 'BEZ.', colBz, hy);
   566→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND', colBl, hy);
   567→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 35, align: 'right' });
   568→  doc.y += 16;
   569→
   570→  participants.forEach((p, i) => {
   571→    ensureSpace(doc, 11);
   572→    const rowY = doc.y;
   573→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   574→      doc.save();
   575→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 10).fill(GRAY_50);
   576→      doc.restore();
   577→    }
   578→    const ty = rowY + 2;
   579→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   580→    textAt(doc, String(i + 1), colNum, ty);
   581→    const suffixStr = p.suffixes ? p.suffixes.split(',').filter(Boolean).map(s => s.startsWith('/') ? s : `/${s}`).join('') : '';
   582→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   583→    textAt(doc, p.callsign + suffixStr, colCall, ty);
   584→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);
   585→    textAt(doc, p.name || '', colName, ty, { width: colRep - colName - 5 });
   586→    textAt(doc, p.home_repeater || '', colRep, ty, { width: colBz - colRep - 5 });
   587→    textAt(doc, p.bezirk_code || '', colBz, ty);
   588→    textAt(doc, p.bundesland_name || '', colBl, ty, { width: colRap - colBl - 5 });
   589→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   590→    textAt(doc, String(p.report_count), colRap, ty, { width: 35, align: 'right' });
   591→    doc.y = rowY + 11;
   592→  });
   593→  doc.y += 4;
   594→}
   595→
   596→function renderFooter(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument) {
   597→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   598→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   599→  ensureSpace(doc, 25);
   600→  doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(m.left, doc.y).lineTo(m.left + usable, doc.y).stroke();
   601→  doc.y += 8;
   602→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   603→  textAt(doc, 'oeradio.at  ·  bosarsa.oeradio.at', m.left, doc.y, { width: usable, align: 'center' });
   604→}

   606→// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────
   607→
   608→export function generateAggregatedPdf(
   609→  exercises: ExerciseSummary[],
   610→  stats: PdfStats,
   611→  from: string,
   612→  to: string,
   613→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   614→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   615→    layout: 'landscape',
   616→    size: 'A4',
   617→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   618→    autoFirstPage: true,
   619→    bufferPages: true,
   620→  });
   621→
   622→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${formatDateDE(from)} – ${formatDateDE(to)}  ·  ${exercises.length} Übung${exercises.length !== 1 ? 'en' : ''}`);
   623→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats, exercises.length);
   624→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   625→  renderExerciseTable(doc, exercises);
   626→
   627→  // Page 2: Austria map
   628→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   629→    doc.addPage();
   630→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   631→  }
   632→
   633→  // Page 3: Charts (always BL-level for readability, max 9 entries)
   634→  const chartData = stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   635→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   636→    doc.addPage();
   637→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   638→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   639→  }
   640→
   641→  // Page 4+: Bezirk/BL detail table
   642→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   643→    doc.addPage();
   644→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   645→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   646→    doc.addPage();
   647→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   648→  }
   649→
   650→  // Page 5+: Participants
   651→  doc.addPage();
   652→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   653→  renderFooter(doc);
   654→
   655→  doc.end();
   656→  return doc;
   657→}
   658→
   659→export function generateExercisePdf(
   660→  exercise: { name: string; date: string; exercise_type?: string | null },
   661→  stats: PdfStats,
   662→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   663→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   664→    layout: 'landscape',
   665→    size: 'A4',
   666→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   667→    autoFirstPage: true,
   668→    bufferPages: true,
   669→  });
   670→
   671→  const formattedDate = new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', {
   672→    weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',
   673→  });
   674→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${exercise.name} — ${formattedDate}`);
   675→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats);
   676→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   677→
   678→  // Page 2: Austria map
   679→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   680→    doc.addPage();
   681→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   682→  }
   683→
   684→  // Page 3: Charts (always BL-level for readability, max 9 entries)
   685→  const chartData = stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   686→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   687→    doc.addPage();
   688→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   689→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   690→  }
   691→
   692→  // Page 4+: Bezirk/BL detail table
   693→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   694→    doc.addPage();
   695→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   696→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   697→    doc.addPage();
   698→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   699→  }
   700→
   701→  // Page 5+: Participants
   702→  doc.addPage();
   703→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   704→  renderFooter(doc);
   705→
   706→  doc.end();
   707→  return doc;
   708→}

    60→  const downloadChart = async (cardRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, filename: string) => {
    61→    if (!cardRef.current || !exercise) return;
    62→    setExporting(true);
    63→    const header = createExportHeader();

    68→    const parent = cardRef.current.parentNode!;

    74→    try {

    38→function formatDateDE(d: string) {
    39→  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
    40→  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
    41→}
    42→
    43→function toISO(d: Date) {
    44→  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    45→}
    46→
    47→export default function AggregatedReportsPage() {
    48→  const now = new Date();
    49→  const year = now.getFullYear();
    50→
    51→  const [from, setFrom] = useState(() => toISO(new Date(year, 0, 1)));

    66→
    67→  const fetchStats = (f: string, t: string, types?: Set<string>) => {
    68→    setLoading(true);

    70→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/reports/stats?from=${f}&to=${t}${typesParam}`)
    71→      .then((res) => setData(res))
    72→      .catch(() => setData(null))
    73→      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    74→  };
    75→
    76→  useEffect(() => {

    91→

   651→              >
   652→                {exporting ? 'Exportieren...' : 'Download Auswertung'}
   653→              </button>
   654→              <button
   655→                onClick={async () => {

   658→                    const res = await fetch(`/api/v1/reports/adif?from=${from}&to=${to}`, {
   659→                      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
   660→                    });
   661→                    if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
   662→                    const blob = await res.blob();
   663→                    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
   664→                    const link = document.createElement('a');
   665→                    link.href = url;
   666→                    link.download = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   667→                    link.click();
   668→                    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   669→                  } catch {}
   670→                }}
   671→                className="bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   672→              >
   673→                Download ADIF
   674→              </button>
   675→            </div>
   676→            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-2">

     1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     2→import { apiFetch } from '../services/api';
     3→
     4→export default function OperatorsPage() {
     5→  const [operators, setOperators] = useState<any[]>([]);
     6→  const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);
     7→  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
     8→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
     9→  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<any>(null);
    10→  const [showAdd, setShowAdd] = useState(false);
    11→  const [newOp, setNewOp] = useState({ callsign: '', name: '', qth: '', bezirk_code: '', bundesland_code: '', home_repeater: '' });
    12→  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
    13→  const debounceRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
    14→
    15→  const load = async (q?: string, offset = 0) => {
    16→    setLoading(true);
    17→    try {
    18→      if (q) {
    19→        const results = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);
    20→        setOperators(results);
    21→        setTotal(results.length);
    22→      } else {
    23→        const data = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators?limit=50&offset=${offset}`);
    24→        setOperators(data.operators);
    25→        setTotal(data.total);
    26→      }
    27→    } catch {} finally { setLoading(false); }
    28→  };
    29→
    30→  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);
    31→
    32→  const handleSearch = (q: string) => {
    33→    setSearch(q);
    34→    if (debounceRef.current) clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
    35→    debounceRef.current = setTimeout(() => load(q), 300);
    36→  };
    37→
    38→  const handleSave = async (op: any) => {
    39→    try {
    40→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${op.id}`, {
    41→        method: 'PATCH',
    42→        body: JSON.stringify(op),
    43→      });
    44→      setOperators(prev => prev.map(o => o.id === updated.id ? updated : o));
    45→      setEditing(null);
    46→    } catch {}
    47→  };
    48→
    49→  const handleAdd = async () => {
    50→    try {
    51→      const created = await apiFetch('/api/v1/operators', {
    52→        method: 'POST',
    53→        body: JSON.stringify(newOp),
    54→      });
    55→      setOperators(prev => [created, ...prev]);
    56→      setShowAdd(false);
    57→      setNewOp({ callsign: '', name: '', qth: '', bezirk_code: '', bundesland_code: '', home_repeater: '' });
    58→    } catch (e: any) {
    59→      alert(e.message);
    60→    }
    61→  };
    62→
    63→  const handleDelete = async (op: any) => {
    64→    if (!confirm(`Rufzeichen ${op.callsign} wirklich löschen?`)) return;
    65→    try {
    66→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${op.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    67→      setOperators(prev => prev.filter(o => o.id !== op.id));
    68→      setTotal(t => t - 1);
    69→    } catch (e: any) {
    70→      alert(e.message);
    71→    }
    72→  };
    73→
    74→  const handlePage = (dir: number) => {
    75→    const newPage = page + dir;
    76→    setPage(newPage);
    77→    load(undefined, newPage * 50);
    78→  };
    79→
    80→  return (
    81→    <div className="space-y-4">
    82→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
    83→        <h1 className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">Rufzeichen-Register</h1>
    84→        <button onClick={() => setShowAdd(true)} className="bg-[#c8102e] hover:bg-[#a00d24] text-white px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs sm:text-sm flex-shrink-0">
    85→          + Neu
    86→        </button>
    87→      </div>
    88→
    89→      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
    90→        <input
    91→          type="text"
    92→          value={search}
    93→          onChange={e => handleSearch(e.target.value)}
    94→          placeholder="Rufzeichen, Name oder QTH suchen..."
    95→          className="flex-1 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
    96→          autoFocus
    97→        />
    98→        <span className="text-sm text-gray-500">{total} Einträge</span>
    99→      </div>
   100→
   101→      {showAdd && (
   102→        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow p-4 space-y-2">
   103→          <h3 className="font-medium text-sm">Neues Rufzeichen anlegen</h3>
   104→          <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
   105→            <input value={newOp.callsign} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, callsign: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))} placeholder="Rufzeichen" className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono uppercase w-28" />
   106→            <input value={newOp.name} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, name: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-32" />
   107→            <input value={newOp.qth} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, qth: e.target.value }))} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-32" />
   108→            <input value={newOp.bezirk_code} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, bezirk_code: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))} placeholder="Bezirk" className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-16 uppercase" />
   109→            <input value={newOp.bundesland_code} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, bundesland_code: e.target.value }))} placeholder="BL" className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-12" />
   110→            <input value={newOp.home_repeater} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, home_repeater: e.target.value }))} placeholder="Repeater" className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-28" />
   111→            <button onClick={handleAdd} className="bg-green-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm">Anlegen</button>
   112→            <button onClick={() => setShowAdd(false)} className="text-gray-500 text-sm">Abbrechen</button>
   113→          </div>
   114→        </div>
   115→      )}
   116→
   117→      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-x-auto">
   118→        <table className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm min-w-[400px]">
   119→          <thead className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white">
   120→            <tr>
   121→              <th className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 text-left">Rufzeichen</th>
   122→              <th className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 text-left">Name</th>
   123→              <th className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 text-left hidden sm:table-cell">QTH</th>
   124→              <th className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 text-left hidden md:table-cell">Repeater</th>
   125→              <th className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 text-left">Bezirk</th>
   126→              <th className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 text-left hidden sm:table-cell">BL</th>
   127→              <th className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 text-left hidden md:table-cell">Ausstattung</th>
   128→              <th className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-2"></th>
   129→            </tr>
   130→          </thead>
   131→          <tbody>
   132→            {operators.map(op => (
   133→              <tr key={op.id} className="border-t hover:bg-blue-50">
   134→                {editing?.id === op.id ? (
   135→                  <>
   136→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.callsign} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, callsign: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-24 uppercase" /></td>
   137→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.name || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, name: e.target.value })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24" /></td>
   138→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.qth || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, qth: e.target.value })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24" /></td>
   139→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.home_repeater || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, home_repeater: e.target.value })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24" /></td>
   140→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.bezirk_code || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, bezirk_code: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-12 uppercase" /></td>
   141→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.bundesland_code || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, bundesland_code: e.target.value })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-10" /></td>
   142→                    <td className="px-3 py-1"><input value={editing.equipment || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, equipment: e.target.value })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24" /></td>
   143→                    <td className="px-3 py-1 flex gap-1">
   144→                      <button onClick={() => handleSave(editing)} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   145→                      <button onClick={() => setEditing(null)} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   146→                    </td>
   147→                  </>
   148→                ) : (
   149→                  <>
   150→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 font-mono font-medium">{op.callsign}</td>
   151→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5">{op.name}</td>
   152→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 text-gray-500 hidden sm:table-cell">{op.qth}</td>
   153→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 text-gray-500 text-xs hidden md:table-cell">{op.home_repeater}</td>
   154→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5">
   155→                      {op.bezirk_code && (
   156→                        <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1">{op.bezirk_code}</span>
   157→                      )}
   158→                    </td>
   159→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 text-gray-500 hidden sm:table-cell">{op.bundesland_code}</td>
   160→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-gray-400 hidden md:table-cell">{op.equipment}</td>
   161→                    <td className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 flex gap-2">
   162→                      <button onClick={() => setEditing({ ...op })} className="text-blue-700 hover:underline text-xs">
   163→                        Bearb.
   164→                      </button>
   165→                      <button onClick={() => handleDelete(op)} className="text-red-500 hover:underline text-xs">
   166→                        Löschen
   167→                      </button>
   168→                    </td>
   169→                  </>
   170→                )}
   171→              </tr>
   172→            ))}
   173→          </tbody>
   174→        </table>
   175→      </div>
   176→
   177→      {!search && (
   178→        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4">
   179→          <button onClick={() => handlePage(-1)} disabled={page === 0} className="text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 disabled:opacity-30">
   180→            Zurück
   181→          </button>
   182→          <span className="text-sm text-gray-400">Seite {page + 1}</span>
   183→          <button onClick={() => handlePage(1)} disabled={operators.length < 50} className="text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 disabled:opacity-30">
   184→            Weiter
   185→          </button>
   186→        </div>
   187→      )}
   188→    </div>
   189→  );
   190→}
   191→
