     1→import { Server, Socket } from 'socket.io';
     2→import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
     3→import { JWT_SECRET } from '../middleware/auth.js';
     4→
     5→const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
     6→
     7→export function setupSocket(io: Server): void {
     8→  io.use((socket, next) => {
     9→    const token = socket.handshake.auth?.token;
    10→    if (!token) return next(new Error('Nicht autorisiert'));
    11→    try {
    12→      const payload = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET) as any;
    13→      (socket as any).admin = payload;
    14→      next();
    15→    } catch {
    16→      next(new Error('Token ungültig'));
    17→    }
    18→  });
    19→
    20→  io.on('connection', (socket: Socket) => {
    21→    const admin = (socket as any).admin;
    22→    console.log(`Socket connected: ${admin.username}`);
    23→
    24→    socket.on('join_exercise', ({ exercise_id }) => {
    25→      if (typeof exercise_id !== 'string' || !UUID_RE.test(exercise_id)) return;
    26→      socket.join(`exercise:${exercise_id}`);
    27→      console.log(`${admin.username} joined exercise ${exercise_id}`);
    28→    });
    29→
    30→    socket.on('leave_exercise', ({ exercise_id }) => {
    31→      if (typeof exercise_id !== 'string' || !UUID_RE.test(exercise_id)) return;
    32→      socket.leave(`exercise:${exercise_id}`);
    33→    });
    34→
    35→    socket.on('report_created', (data) => {
    36→      if (typeof data?.exercise_id !== 'string' || !UUID_RE.test(data.exercise_id)) return;
    37→      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('report_created', {
    38→        ...data,
    39→        entered_by: admin.username,
    40→      });
    41→    });
    42→
    43→    socket.on('report_updated', (data) => {
    44→      if (typeof data?.exercise_id !== 'string' || !UUID_RE.test(data.exercise_id)) return;
    45→      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('report_updated', {
    46→        ...data,
    47→        entered_by: admin.username,
    48→      });
    49→    });
    50→
    51→    socket.on('report_deleted', (data) => {
    52→      if (typeof data?.exercise_id !== 'string' || !UUID_RE.test(data.exercise_id)) return;
    53→      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('report_deleted', {
    54→        ...data,
    55→        entered_by: admin.username,
    56→      });
    57→    });
    58→
    59→    socket.on('op_callsign_updated', (data) => {
    60→      if (typeof data?.exercise_id !== 'string' || !UUID_RE.test(data.exercise_id)) return;
    61→      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('op_callsign_updated', data);
    62→    });
    63→
    64→    socket.on('attendance_updated', (data) => {
    65→      if (typeof data?.exercise_id !== 'string' || !UUID_RE.test(data.exercise_id)) return;
    66→      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('attendance_updated', {
    67→        ...data,
    68→        entered_by: admin.username,
    69→      });
    70→    });
    71→
    72→    socket.on('disconnect', () => {
    73→      console.log(`Socket disconnected: ${admin.username}`);
    74→    });
    75→  });
    76→}
    77→

     1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef, type FormEvent } from 'react';
     2→import { apiFetch, getOrCreateOperator } from '../services/api';
     3→import { getSocket } from '../services/socket';
     4→import CallsignInput, { type CallsignInputRef } from './CallsignInput';
     5→
     6→interface Props {
     7→  exerciseId: string;
     8→  reports: any[];
     9→  onReportCreated: (report: any) => void;
    10→  onReportUpdated: (report: any) => void;
    11→  onReportDeleted: (reportId: number) => void;
    12→}
    13→
    14→interface EntryForm {
    15→  callsign: string;
    16→  operator: any;
    17→  rapport: string;
    18→  notes: string;
    19→  opName: string;
    20→  opQth: string;
    21→  bezirkCode: string;
    22→  suffix?: string;
    23→}
    24→
    25→const SUFFIXES = ['/m', '/p', '/am', '/mm'] as const;
    26→
    27→export default function BundMode({ exerciseId, reports, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {
    28→  const [bundeslaender, setBundeslaender] = useState<any[]>([]);
    29→  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);
    30→  const [linkedRepeaters, setLinkedRepeaters] = useState<any[]>([]);
    31→  const [blRepSelection, setBlRepSelection] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
    32→  const [blOpCallsigns, setBlOpCallsigns] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
    33→  const [collapsedBl, setCollapsedBl] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    34→  const [addRepBl, setAddRepBl] = useState<string | null>(null);
    35→  const [newRepName, setNewRepName] = useState('');
    36→  const [newRepFreq, setNewRepFreq] = useState('');
    37→  const [newRepCallsign, setNewRepCallsign] = useState('');
    38→  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    39→  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
    40→  const [dragReportId, setDragReportId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    41→
    42→  useEffect(() => {
    43→    Promise.all([
    44→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bundeslaender'),
    45→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke'),
    46→      apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters'),
    47→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`),
    48→    ]).then(([bl, bz, allReps, exReps]) => {
    49→      setBundeslaender(bl);
    50→      setBezirke(bz);
    51→      const linked = allReps.filter((r: any) => r.is_linked);
    52→      setLinkedRepeaters(linked);
    53→      // Initialize per-BL defaults: first linked in that BL, else first overall
    54→      const defaults: Record<string, number> = {};
    55→      const ops: Record<string, string> = {};
    56→      for (const b of bl) {
    57→        const inBl = linked.find((r: any) => r.bundesland_code === b.code);
    58→        const repId = inBl ? inBl.id : (linked[0]?.id ?? 0);
    59→        defaults[b.code] = repId;
    60→        // Load existing OP callsign from exercise_repeaters
    61→        const exRep = exReps.find((er: any) => er.repeater_id === repId);
    62→        ops[b.code] = exRep?.operator_callsign || '';
    63→      }
    64→      setBlRepSelection(defaults);
    65→      setBlOpCallsigns(ops);
    66→    }).catch(() => {});
    67→
    68→    const socket = getSocket();
    69→    const handleOpCallsignUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; operator_callsign: string }) => {
    70→      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;
    71→      // Map repeater_id back to blCode via current blRepSelection
    72→      setBlRepSelection(sel => {
    73→        for (const [blCode, repId] of Object.entries(sel)) {
    74→          if (repId === data.repeater_id) {
    75→            setBlOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [blCode]: data.operator_callsign || '' }));
    76→          }
    77→        }
    78→        return sel;
    79→      });
    80→    };
    81→    socket.on('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);
    82→    return () => { socket.off('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated); };
    83→  }, [exerciseId]);
    84→
    85→  const saveOpCallsign = async (blCode: string, val: string) => {
    86→    const repId = blRepSelection[blCode];
    87→    if (!repId) return;
    88→    try {
    89→      // Ensure exercise_repeater exists, then update
    90→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`, {
    91→        method: 'POST',
    92→        body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: repId, operator_callsign: val || null }),
    93→      });
    94→    } catch {
    95→      // Already exists, just patch
    96→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters/${repId}`, {
    97→        method: 'PATCH',
    98→        body: JSON.stringify({ operator_callsign: val || null }),
    99→      });
   100→    }
   101→    getSocket().emit('op_callsign_updated', { exercise_id: exerciseId, repeater_id: repId, operator_callsign: val });
   102→  };
   103→
   104→  const addRepeater = async (blCode: string) => {
   105→    if (!newRepName && !newRepFreq) return;
   106→    const name = newRepName || `Direkte ${newRepFreq}`;
   107→    const freq = newRepFreq ? parseFloat(newRepFreq) : null;
   108→    try {
   109→      const rep = await apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters', {
   110→        method: 'POST',
   111→        body: JSON.stringify({
   112→          short_name: name,
   113→          site_name: name,
   114→          frequency_mhz: freq,
   115→          callsign: newRepCallsign || null,
   116→          band: freq ? (freq > 400 ? '70cm' : freq > 200 ? '23cm' : '2m') : null,
   117→          type: 'repeater',
   118→          bundesland_code: blCode,
   119→        }),
   120→      });
   121→      setLinkedRepeaters(prev => [...prev, rep]);
   122→      setNewRepName('');
   123→      setNewRepFreq('');
   124→      setNewRepCallsign('');
   125→      setAddRepBl(null);
   126→    } catch {}
   127→  };
   128→
   129→  const toggleBl = (code: string) => {
   130→    setCollapsedBl(prev => {
   131→      const next = new Set(prev);
   132→      if (next.has(code)) next.delete(code); else next.add(code);
   133→      return next;
   134→    });
   135→  };
   136→
   137→  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {
   138→    const match = raw.match(/^(\d)\/(\d)(.*)$/);
   139→    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };
   140→    return {
   141→      readability: parseInt(match[1]),
   142→      strength: parseInt(match[2]),
   143→      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,
   144→    };
   145→  };
   146→
   147→  const handleBezirkSubmit = async (bezirkCode: string, repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => {
   148→    try {
   149→      if (!repeaterId) return;
   150→      const operator = await getOrCreateOperator(form.callsign, form.operator);
   151→      if (!operator) return;
   152→
   153→      const parsed = parseRapport(form.rapport);
   154→
   155→      try {
   156→        const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
   157→          method: 'POST',
   158→          body: JSON.stringify({
   159→            operator_id: operator.id,
   160→            repeater_id: repeaterId,
   161→            ...parsed,
   162→            notes: form.notes || null,
   163→            bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null,
   164→            suffix: form.suffix || null,
   165→          }),
   166→        });
   167→        onReportCreated(report);
   168→      } catch (err: any) {
   169→        if (err.message?.includes('existiert') && err.data?.existing_report) {
   170→          const existing = err.data.existing_report;
   171→          const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${existing.id}`, {
   172→            method: 'PATCH',
   173→            body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: form.notes || null, bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null, suffix: form.suffix || null }),
   174→          });
   175→          onReportUpdated(updated);
   176→        }
   177→      }
   178→    } catch {
   179→    }
   180→  };
   181→
   182→  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {
   183→    setEditingId(report.id);
   184→    setEditForm({
   185→      callsign: report.callsign,
   186→      operator: { id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign, name: report.operator_name || null, qth: report.operator_qth || null },
   187→      rapport: report.readability && report.strength ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '',
   188→      notes: report.notes || '',
   189→      opName: report.operator_name || '',
   190→      opQth: report.operator_qth || '',
   191→      bezirkCode: report.bezirk_code || '',
   192→      suffix: report.suffix || '',
   193→    });
   194→  };
   195→
   196→  const handleUpdate = async () => {
   197→    if (!editingId) return;
   198→    const parsed = parseRapport(editForm.rapport);
   199→    try {
   200→      // Save operator name/qth if changed
   201→      if (editForm.operator?.id) {
   202→        const nameChanged = (editForm.opName || '') !== (editForm.operator.name || '');
   203→        const qthChanged = (editForm.opQth || '') !== (editForm.operator.qth || '');
   204→        if (nameChanged || qthChanged) {
   205→          const patch: any = {};
   206→          if (nameChanged) patch.name = editForm.opName || null;
   207→          if (qthChanged) patch.qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   208→          await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${editForm.operator.id}`, {
   209→            method: 'PATCH',
   210→            body: JSON.stringify(patch),
   211→          });
   212→        }
   213→      }
   214→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {
   215→        method: 'PATCH',
   216→        body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: editForm.notes || null, bezirk_code: editForm.bezirkCode || null, suffix: editForm.suffix || null }),
   217→      });
   218→      if (editForm.opName !== undefined) updated.operator_name = editForm.opName || null;
   219→      if (editForm.opQth !== undefined) updated.operator_qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   220→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   221→      setEditingId(null);
   222→    } catch {}
   223→  };
   224→
   225→  const handleMoveToBezirk = async (reportId: number, newBezirkCode: string | null) => {
   226→    try {
   227→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, {
   228→        method: 'PATCH',
   229→        body: JSON.stringify({ bezirk_code: newBezirkCode }),
   230→      });
   231→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   232→    } catch {}
   233→  };
   234→
   235→  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {
   236→    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;
   237→    try {
   238→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   239→      onReportDeleted(reportId);
   240→    } catch {}
   241→  };
   242→
   243→  const domesticBlCodes = new Set(bundeslaender.filter(b => !b.is_international).map(b => b.code));
   244→
   245→  return (
   246→    <div className="space-y-2">
   247→      {bundeslaender.filter(bl => !bl.is_international).map(bl => {
   248→        const blBezirke = bezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === bl.code);
   249→        const defaultRepId = blRepSelection[bl.code] || 0;
   250→        // Match reports by bezirk's Bundesland, only for linked repeaters
   251→        const blBezirkCodes = new Set(blBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   252→        const blReports = reports.filter(r => {
   253→          if (!r.repeater_is_linked) return false;
   254→          // Primary: match by bezirk code
   255→          if (r.bezirk_code && blBezirkCodes.has(r.bezirk_code)) return true;
   256→          // Fallback for null bezirk_code: use operator BL (if domestic), else repeater BL
   257→          if (!r.bezirk_code) {
   258→            const effectiveBl = domesticBlCodes.has(r.bundesland_code) ? r.bundesland_code : r.repeater_bundesland_code;
   259→            return effectiveBl === bl.code;
   260→          }
   261→          return false;
   262→        });
   263→        const isCollapsed = collapsedBl.has(bl.code);
   264→        const reportCount = blReports.filter(r => !r.is_op_marker && r.readability).length;
   265→
   266→        return (
   267→          <div key={bl.code} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm">
   268→            <div className="bg-gray-50 px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b gap-1">
   269→              <div
   270→                onClick={() => toggleBl(bl.code)}
   271→                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 rounded px-1 -mx-1 min-w-0"
   272→              >
   273→                <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{isCollapsed ? '▶' : '▼'}</span>
   274→                <span className="font-medium text-xs sm:text-sm truncate">OE{parseInt(bl.code)} {bl.name}</span>
   275→                <span className="bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full flex-shrink-0">{reportCount}</span>
   276→              </div>
   277→              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
   278→                <button
   279→                  onClick={() => setAddRepBl(addRepBl === bl.code ? null : bl.code)}
   280→                  className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 hidden sm:inline"
   281→                >
   282→                  + Umsetzer
   283→                </button>
   284→                <label className={`text-xs ${blOpCallsigns[bl.code] ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-[#c8102e] font-semibold'}`}>OP:</label>
   285→                <input
   286→                  type="text"
   287→                  value={blOpCallsigns[bl.code] || ''}
   288→                  onChange={e => setBlOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [bl.code]: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   289→                  onBlur={e => saveOpCallsign(bl.code, e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   290→                  placeholder="Rufz."
   291→                  className={`border rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 ${blOpCallsigns[bl.code] ? 'border-gray-300' : 'border-[#c8102e] placeholder-[#c8102e]/60'}`}
   292→                />
   293→              </div>
   294→            </div>
   295→
   296→            {!isCollapsed && (
   297→              <div>
   298→                {addRepBl === bl.code && (
   299→                  <div className="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-50 border-b flex items-center gap-1 flex-wrap">
   300→                    <input
   301→                      type="text"
   302→                      value={newRepName}
   303→                      onChange={e => setNewRepName(e.target.value)}
   304→                      placeholder="Name"
   305→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-28 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   306→                      autoFocus
   307→                    />
   308→                    <input
   309→                      type="text"
   310→                      value={newRepFreq}
   311→                      onChange={e => setNewRepFreq(e.target.value)}
   312→                      placeholder="MHz"
   313→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   314→                    />
   315→                    <input
   316→                      type="text"
   317→                      value={newRepCallsign}
   318→                      onChange={e => setNewRepCallsign(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   319→                      placeholder="Rufz."
   320→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   321→                    />
   322→                    <button
   323→                      onClick={() => addRepeater(bl.code)}
   324→                      className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs"
   325→                    >
   326→                      OK
   327→                    </button>
   328→                    <button
   329→                      onClick={() => { setAddRepBl(null); setNewRepName(''); setNewRepFreq(''); setNewRepCallsign(''); }}
   330→                      className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-xs"
   331→                    >
   332→                      Abbrechen
   333→                    </button>
   334→                  </div>
   335→                )}
   336→                <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   337→                  {blBezirke.map(bz => {
   338→                    const bzReports = blReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   339→                    return (
   340→                      <BezirkRow
   341→                        key={bz.code}
   342→                        bezirk={bz}
   343→                        reports={bzReports}
   344→                        availableBezirke={blBezirke}
   345→                        linkedRepeaters={linkedRepeaters}
   346→                        defaultRepeaterId={defaultRepId}
   347→                        onSubmit={(repeaterId, form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, repeaterId, form)}
   348→                        onEdit={handleEdit}
   349→                        onDelete={handleDelete}
   350→                        onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   351→                        editingId={editingId}
   352→                        editForm={editForm}
   353→                        onEditChange={setEditForm}
   354→                        onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   355→                        onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   356→                        dragReportId={dragReportId}
   357→                        onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   358→                        onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   359→                      />
   360→                    );
   361→                  })}
   362→                  {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk + entry form */}
   363→                  {(() => {
   364→                    const bzCodes = new Set(blBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   365→                    const unassigned = blReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   366→                    return (
   367→                      <BezirkRow
   368→                        bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}
   369→                        reports={unassigned}
   370→                        availableBezirke={blBezirke}
   371→                        linkedRepeaters={linkedRepeaters}
   372→                        defaultRepeaterId={defaultRepId}
   373→                        onSubmit={(repeaterId, form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', repeaterId, form)}
   374→                        onEdit={handleEdit}
   375→                        onDelete={handleDelete}
   376→                        onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   377→                        editingId={editingId}
   378→                        editForm={editForm}
   379→                        onEditChange={setEditForm}
   380→                        onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   381→                        onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   382→                        dragReportId={dragReportId}
   383→                        onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   384→                        onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   385→                      />
   386→                    );
   387→                  })()}
   388→                </div>
   389→              </div>
   390→            )}
   391→          </div>
   392→        );
   393→      })}
   394→    </div>
   395→  );
   396→}
   397→
   398→interface BezirkRowProps {
   399→  bezirk: any;
   400→  reports: any[];
   401→  availableBezirke: any[];
   402→  linkedRepeaters: any[];
   403→  defaultRepeaterId: number;
   404→  onSubmit: (repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => void;
   405→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   406→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   407→  onMoveToBezirk: (reportId: number, bezirkCode: string | null) => void;
   408→  editingId: number | null;
   409→  editForm: EntryForm;
   410→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   411→  onEditSave: () => void;
   412→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   413→  dragReportId: number | null;
   414→  onDragStart: (reportId: number) => void;
   415→  onDragEnd: () => void;
   416→}
   417→
   418→function BezirkRow({ bezirk, reports, availableBezirke, linkedRepeaters, defaultRepeaterId, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, onMoveToBezirk, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel, dragReportId, onDragStart, onDragEnd }: BezirkRowProps) {
   419→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
   420→  const [repeaterId, setRepeaterId] = useState(defaultRepeaterId);
   421→  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
   422→  const callsignRef = useRef<CallsignInputRef>(null);
   423→  const rapportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   424→
   425→  useEffect(() => {
   426→    if (defaultRepeaterId && !repeaterId) setRepeaterId(defaultRepeaterId);
   427→  }, [defaultRepeaterId]);
   428→
   429→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   430→    e.preventDefault();
   431→    if (!form.callsign && !form.operator) return;
   432→    if (!repeaterId) return;
   433→    onSubmit(repeaterId, form);
   434→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
   435→    setTimeout(() => callsignRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   436→  };
   437→
   438→  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
   439→    e.preventDefault();
   440→    setDragOver(false);
   441→    if (dragReportId) {
   442→      const targetCode = bezirk.code === '??' ? null : bezirk.code;
   443→      onMoveToBezirk(dragReportId, targetCode);
   444→    }
   445→  };
   446→
   447→  const saveOperatorField = (field: 'name' | 'qth', value: string) => {
   448→    if (!form.operator?.id) return;
   449→    const original = field === 'name' ? (form.operator.name || '') : (form.operator.qth || '');
   450→    if (value === original) return;
   451→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${form.operator.id}`, {
   452→      method: 'PATCH',
   453→      body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value || null }),
   454→    }).then(() => {
   455→      setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: { ...f.operator, [field]: value || null } }));
   456→    }).catch(() => {});
   457→  };
   458→
   459→  return (
   460→    <div
   461→      className={`px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors ${dragOver && dragReportId ? 'bg-blue-100 ring-2 ring-blue-400 ring-inset' : ''}`}
   462→      onDragOver={e => { if (dragReportId) { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); } }}
   463→      onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
   464→      onDrop={handleDrop}
   465→    >
   466→      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 mb-1">
   467→        <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bezirk.code === '??' ? 'bg-[#ffc107] text-gray-800' : bezirk.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   468→          {bezirk.code === '??' ? '??' : bezirk.code}
   469→        </span>
   470→        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{bezirk.name}</span>
   471→        {reports.length > 0 && <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0">({reports.length})</span>}
   472→      </div>
   473→
   474→      {reports.length > 0 && (
   475→        <table className="w-full text-xs mb-1">
   476→          <tbody>
   477→            {reports.map((r, idx) => (
   478→              <tr
   479→                key={r.id}
   480→                className={`hover:bg-blue-50 group ${dragReportId === r.id ? 'opacity-40' : ''}`}
   481→                draggable={editingId !== r.id}
   482→                onDragStart={e => { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; onDragStart(r.id); }}
   483→                onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
   484→              >
   485→                {editingId === r.id ? (
   486→                  <td colSpan={6} className="py-0.5">
   487→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 rounded p-1 flex-wrap" onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); onEditSave(); } }}>
   488→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   489→                      <input value={editForm.opName} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 hidden sm:block" />
   490→                      <input value={editForm.opQth} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opQth: e.target.value })} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16 hidden sm:block" />
   491→                      <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" autoFocus />
   492→                      <input value={editForm.notes} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, notes: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sonst." className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" />
   493→                      <select
   494→                        value={editForm.bezirkCode}
   495→                        onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, bezirkCode: e.target.value })}
   496→                        className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16"
   497→                        title="Bezirk"
   498→                      >
   499→                        <option value="">??</option>
   500→                        {availableBezirke.map(bz => <option key={bz.code} value={bz.code}>{bz.code}</option>)}
   501→                      </select>
   502→                      <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   503→                      <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   504→                    </div>
   505→                  </td>
   506→                ) : (
   507→                  <>
   508→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-400 w-4 cursor-grab">{idx + 1}</td>
   509→                    <td className="py-0.5 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   510→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}{r.suffix || ''}</span>
   511→                      {r.operator_name && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs">{r.operator_name}</span>}
   512→                      {r.operator_qth && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400 text-xs hidden sm:inline">{r.operator_qth}</span>}
   513→                    </td>
   514→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-blue-600 text-xs cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.repeater_name || ''}</td>
   515→                    <td className="py-0.5 font-mono text-gray-500 w-16 sm:w-24 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   516→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}
   517→                    </td>
   518→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-500 cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.notes || ''}</td>
   519→                    <td className="py-0.5 w-4">
   520→                      <button onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">x</button>
   521→                    </td>
   522→                  </>
   523→                )}
   524→              </tr>
   525→            ))}
   526→          </tbody>
   527→        </table>
   528→      )}
   529→
   530→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   531→        {linkedRepeaters.length > 0 && (
   532→          <select
   533→            value={repeaterId || ''}
   534→            onChange={e => setRepeaterId(parseInt(e.target.value))}
   535→            className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs max-w-[80px] sm:max-w-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   536→          >
   537→            {linkedRepeaters.map(r => (
   538→              <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>
   539→                {r.site_name || r.short_name}{r.callsign ? ` (${r.callsign})` : ''}
   540→              </option>
   541→            ))}
   542→          </select>
   543→        )}
   544→        <CallsignInput
   545→          ref={callsignRef}
   546→          value={form.callsign}
   547→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v, operator: null, opName: '', opQth: '' }))}
   548→          onSelect={op => {
   549→            setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign, opName: op.name || '', opQth: op.qth || '' }));
   550→            setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50);
   551→          }}
   552→          onEnter={() => setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50)}
   553→          className="w-24 sm:w-28"
   554→        />
   555→        <div className="flex gap-0.5">
   556→          {SUFFIXES.map(s => (
   557→            <button
   558→              key={s}
   559→              type="button"
   560→              onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, suffix: f.suffix === s ? '' : s }))}
   561→              className={`px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono border ${form.suffix === s ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200'}`}
   562→              title={s}
   563→            >
   564→              {s.slice(1)}
   565→            </button>
   566→          ))}
   567→        </div>
   568→        <input
   569→          type="text"
   570→          value={form.opName}
   571→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opName: e.target.value }))}
   572→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('name', e.target.value)}
   573→          placeholder="Name"
   574→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   575→        />
   576→        <input
   577→          type="text"
   578→          value={form.opQth}
   579→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opQth: e.target.value }))}
   580→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('qth', e.target.value)}
   581→          placeholder="QTH"
   582→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   583→        />
   584→        <input
   585→          ref={rapportRef}
   586→          type="text"
   587→          value={form.rapport}
   588→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   589→          placeholder="5/9"
   590→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   591→          onKeyDown={e => {
   592→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   593→              e.preventDefault();
   594→              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));
   595→            }
   596→          }}
   597→        />
   598→        <input
   599→          type="text"
   600→          value={form.notes}
   601→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}
   602→          placeholder="Sonst."
   603→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs flex-1 min-w-[40px] sm:min-w-[60px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   604→        />
   605→        <button type="submit" title="Rapport speichern (Enter)" className="bg-[#c8102e] hover:bg-[#a00d24] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs flex-shrink-0">
   606→          + ↵
   607→        </button>
   608→      </form>
   609→    </div>
   610→  );
   611→}
   612→

{

WHERE exercise_id = ? AND repeater_id = ?')\n    .run(operator_callsign || null, req.params.id, req.params.rid);\n  res.json({ success: true });\n});",
  "new_string": "exercisesRouter.patch('/:id/repeaters/:rid', (req, res) => {\n  const db = getDb();\n  const updates: string[] = [];\n  const params: any[] = [];\n  if (req.body.operator_callsign !== undefined) { updates.push('operator_callsign = ?'); params.push(req.body.operator_callsign || null); }\n  if (req.body.home_repeater !== undefined) { updates.push('home_repeater = ?'); params.push(req.body.home_repeater || null); }\n  if (updates.length === 0) {\n    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Keine Änderungen' });\n    return;\n  }\n  params.push(req.params.id, req.params.rid);\n  db.prepare(`UPDATE exercise_repeaters SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE exercise_id = ? AND repeater_id = ?`)\n    .run(...params);\n  res.json({ success: true });\n});"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/BundMode.tsx",
  "old_string": "    const socket = getSocket();\n    const handleOpCallsignUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; operator_callsign: string }) => {\n      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;\n      // Map repeater_id back to blCode via current blRepSelection\n      setBlRepSelection(sel => {\n        for (const [blCode, repId] of Object.entries(sel)) {\n          if (repId === data.repeater_id) {\n            setBlOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [blCode]: data.operator_callsign || '' }));\n          }\n        }\n        return sel;\n      });\n    };\n    socket.on('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);\n    return () => { socket.off('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated); };",
  "new_string": "    const socket = getSocket();\n    const handleOpCallsignUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; operator_callsign: string }) => {\n      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;\n      setBlRepSelection(sel => {\n        for (const [blCode, repId] of Object.entries(sel)) {\n          if (repId === data.repeater_id) {\n            setBlOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [blCode]: data.operator_callsign || '' }));\n          }\n        }\n        return sel;\n      });\n    };\n    const handleHomeRepeaterUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; home_repeater: string }) => {\n      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;\n      setBlRepSelection(sel => {\n        for (const [blCode, repId] of Object.entries(sel)) {\n          if (repId === data.repeater_id) {\n            setBlHomeRepeaters(prev => ({ ...prev, [blCode]: data.home_repeater || '' }));\n          }\n        }\n        return sel;\n      });\n    };\n    socket.on('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);\n    socket.on('home_repeater_updated', handleHomeRepeaterUpdated);\n    return () => {\n      socket.off('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);\n      socket.off('home_repeater_updated', handleHomeRepeaterUpdated);\n    };"
}

**Part 1 - Reverted home_repeater from Stammdaten/Auswertung (9 files):**
- `server/src/routes/operators.ts` — Removed `home_repeater` from POST destructuring/INSERT and PATCH fields
- `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` — Removed `o.home_repeater` from stats participants query
- `server/src/routes/reports.ts` — Removed `o.home_repeater` from participants query
- `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` — Removed `o.home_repeater` from both participants queries
- `server/src/services/pdf.ts` — Removed `home_repeater` from Participant interface; reverted renderParticipants (removed REP column, restored Name width)
- `client/src/types/stats.ts` — Removed `home_repeater` from Participant interface
- `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` — Removed Repeater th/td from participants table
- `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` — Removed Repeater th/td from participants table
- `client/src/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx` — Removed Repeater column from header, display, edit, and add form

**Part 2 - New implementation in BundMode (4 files):**
- `server/src/db/schema.ts` — Added migration: `ALTER TABLE exercise_repeaters ADD COLUMN home_repeater TEXT`
- `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` — POST accepts `home_repeater`, PATCH supports partial updates for both `operator_callsign` and `home_repeater`
- `server/src/services/socket.ts` — Added `home_repeater_updated` socket handler
- `client/src/components/BundMode.tsx` — Added `blHomeRepeaters` state, load from exercise_repeaters, `saveHomeRepeater()` function (POST-then-PATCH pattern), socket listener for sync, and REP input next to OP input in the BL header bar

     5→### #1 — Rapport soll verschiebbar innerhalb der Bezirke sein
     6→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/1
     7→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
     8→- **Umsetzung:**
     9→  - **Bezirk-Dropdown im Bearbeitungsmodus:** Beim Klicken auf einen Rapport erscheint ein Bezirk-Auswahlfeld neben Rapport, Notizen usw. Bezirk wechseln und mit OK bestätigen.
    10→  - **Drag & Drop:** Rapport-Zeilen können per Drag & Drop zwischen Bezirk-Abschnitten verschoben werden. Das Ziel wird blau hervorgehoben. Funktioniert in Frequenzen- und OE-Link-Modus.
    11→  - Dropdown funktioniert auf allen Geräten (inkl. Mobilgeräte), Drag & Drop auf Desktop.
    12→  - Kein Backend-Änderung nötig — der PATCH-Endpunkt unterstützte `bezirk_code` bereits.

    16→- Zusätzliches lokales Backup in `backups/bosarsalog-backup-2026-02-24.db` (1,6 MB, Integritätsprüfung bestanden).

    20→- **Ergebnis:** Kein Datenfehler — die Filterlogik (linked → OE-Link, non-linked → Frequenzen) funktioniert korrekt wie vorgesehen.
    21→

    23→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/2
    24→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed

    26→  - **Neuer Toggle pro Übung:** Admin-only Checkbox "Simplex Bezirke aktivieren" in der Übungs-Einrichtung (nach OE-Link-Toggle). Standardmäßig deaktiviert.
    27→  - **Schema-Migration:** Neue Spalte `simplex_bezirke INTEGER DEFAULT 0` in `exercises`-Tabelle (nicht-destruktiver ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN).
    28→  - **API:** GET/POST/PATCH Exercises-Routen unterstützen `simplex_bezirke`-Feld.

    30→  - Bezirke werden aus den gewählten Bundesländern übernommen.

Admin kann pro Übung auswählen, welche BLs in der Simplex-Gruppierung angezeigt werden. Beim erstmaligen Aktivieren werden alle 9 BLs vorausgewählt. Schema: neue Spalte `simplex_bl_codes TEXT` (komma-separiert, NULL = alle).
    32→  - Drag & Drop zwischen Bezirken funktioniert im gruppierten Modus.

    37→### #4 — Eingabe vom OP Rufzeichen wird nicht in Echtzeit übernommen
    38→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/4
    39→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    40→- **Umsetzung:**
    41→  - **Neues WebSocket-Event `op_callsign_updated`:** Server empfängt das Event und broadcastet es an alle anderen Clients im selben Exercise-Room.
    42→  - **LandMode (Frequenzen-Modus):** Nach dem Speichern eines OP-Rufzeichens wird `op_callsign_updated` emittiert. Eingehende Events aktualisieren `opCallsigns`-State sofort.
    43→  - **BundMode (OE-Link-Modus):** Gleicher Pattern — nach API-Call wird emittiert. Eingehende Events mappen `repeater_id` zurück auf `blCode` via `blRepSelection` und aktualisieren `blOpCallsigns`.
    44→  - Cleanup: Socket-Listener werden beim Unmount/Re-Mount korrekt entfernt.

    49→## Offene Issues
    50→
    51→### #3 — Eigener Repeater pro Operator (Stammdaten)
    52→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/3
    53→- **Status:** Erledigt
    54→- **Umsetzung:**
    55→  - **Schema-Migration:** Neue Spalte `home_repeater TEXT DEFAULT NULL` in `operators`-Tabelle (Freitext, optional).
    56→  - **API:** POST/PATCH Operators akzeptieren `home_repeater`-Feld. GET liefert es automatisch mit `SELECT *`.
    57→  - **Stats-Queries:** Alle 4 Participants-Queries (exercises.ts, reports.ts, pdf.ts x2) selektieren `o.home_repeater`.
    58→  - **Stammdaten UI (OperatorsPage):** Neue Spalte "Repeater" in der Tabelle. Inline-Edit und Add-Form mit Freitext-Input.
    59→  - **Auswertung Web:** Neue Spalte "Repeater" in der Teilnehmer-Liste (ReportsPage + AggregatedReportsPage).
    60→  - **Auswertung PDF:** Neue Spalte "REP." in der Teilnehmer-Liste zwischen Name und Bezirk.
    61→  - Operatoren ohne Repeater: Spalte bleibt leer.
    62→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `schema.ts`, `operators.ts`, `exercises.ts`, `reports.ts`, `pdf.ts` (Routes), `pdf.ts` (Service), `stats.ts`, `OperatorsPage.tsx`, `ReportsPage.tsx`, `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`
    63→
    64→### #5 — Eventuell mehrere OPs bei Simplex
    65→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/5
    66→- **Status:** Geschlossen — bereits möglich
    67→- Mehrere OPs können kommagetrennt im OP-Feld eingetragen werden, z.B. `OE1OP1, OE2OP2`. Das Feld ist ein Freitext-Input ohne Beschränkung auf ein einzelnes Rufzeichen.
    68→
    69→### #8 — Download Auswertung komplett — Server-seitiges PDF
    70→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/8
    71→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    72→- **Problem:** "Download Auswertung komplett" nutzte `html-to-image` (`toPng()`), das den gesamten DOM klonte — inkl. SVG-Karte, Chart.js-Diagramme, große Tabellen. Bei umfangreichen Auswertungen crashte der Browser (500MB+ RAM, Main Thread blockiert).
    73→- **Lösung:** PDF-Generierung auf dem Server mit PDFKit (pure JS, kein Chromium nötig).
    74→- **Umsetzung:**
    75→  - **Neue Datei `server/src/services/pdf.ts`:** PDF-Generator mit Render-Funktionen: Header (Navy-Leiste mit BOS-ARSA Branding), Summary-Cards, Per-Repeater-Grid, Übungstabelle, Austria-Karte (SVG-Pfade direkt via PDFKit gezeichnet mit Heatmap-Farben), Balkendiagramm (Rechtecke), Tortendiagramm (Kreissegmente), Bezirk/BL-Tabelle, Teilnehmer-Liste, Footer.
    76→  - **Neue Datei `server/src/routes/pdf.ts`:** Zwei Endpunkte: `GET /api/v1/pdf/reports?from=...&to=...&types=...` (aggregiert) und `GET /api/v1/pdf/exercises/:id` (Einzel-Übung).
    77→  - **Client:** `downloadFullPage()` in `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` und `ReportsPage.tsx` ruft jetzt den Server-PDF-Endpunkt auf statt `toPng()`. Dateiname: `.pdf` statt `.png`.
    78→  - Einzelne Chart-Downloads (Karte, Balken, Torte) bleiben als PNG via `html-to-image` — **kein Funktionsverlust**.
    79→  - PDF: A4 Querformat, automatische Seitenumbrüche.
    80→- **Nachträgliche Fixes (4 Iterationen):**
    81→  - **Fix 1 — Tabellen-Layout:** `doc.text()` verschob Y-Cursor automatisch bei jeder Spalte → `textAt()`-Helper mit `lineBreak: false` und manueller Y-Steuerung pro Zeile.
    82→  - **Fix 2 — Austria-Karte schwarz:** PDFKit versteht kein CSS `rgb()` → Hex-Format (`#rrggbb`). `.fill().stroke()` Chaining funktioniert nicht → `fillAndStroke()`.
    83→  - **Fix 3 — Kartentext-Kontrast:** Labels bei dunklem Heatmap-Hintergrund (z.B. Kärnten mit vielen Teilnehmern) unsichtbar → `getLabelColor()` berechnet wahrgenommene Helligkeit und wechselt zu weißem Text bei dunklem Hintergrund.
    84→  - **Fix 4 — A4-Layout-Optimierung:**
    85→    - **Alle Repeater angezeigt:** `slice(0, 6)` Limit entfernt, Multi-Row Grid (6 pro Zeile, automatisch umbrechen).
    86→    - **Charts auf BL-Ebene:** Balken-/Tortendiagramm nutzt immer Bundesland-Daten (max 9 Einträge) statt Bezirke (40+) für Lesbarkeit. Bezirk-Detail bleibt in der Tabelle.
    87→    - **Charts auf eigener Seite:** Kein Split durch `ensureSpace` mitten im Chart-Block.
    88→    - **Pie-Legend Safety:** Berechnet verfügbaren Platz, zeigt „… +N weitere" bei Überlauf.
    89→  - **Offen:** Spaltenheader bei Seitenumbrüchen in Bezirk-/Teilnehmer-Tabellen wiederholen.
    90→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `server/src/services/pdf.ts` (NEU), `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` (NEU), `server/src/index.ts`, `server/package.json`, `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`, `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`
    91→
    92→### #9 — Rapporte Verteilung schneidet Bundesland-Namen ab
    93→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/9
    94→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    95→- **Problem:** Balken- und Tortendiagramm in der Webansicht (Auswertung) nutzten volle Bundesland-Namen (z.B. „Niederösterreich") als Labels — werden bei wenig Platz abgeschnitten.
    96→- **Fix:** Labels verwenden jetzt OE-Kurzform: `OE3` (Bundesland-Ebene) bzw. `OE3 AM` (Bezirk-Ebene mit Bezirkscode).
    97→- **Scope:** Nur Webansicht (Client). PDF-Labels waren bereits auf BL-Ebene.
    98→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`, `ReportsPage.tsx`
    99→
   100→### #10 — Suffix-Checkboxen (/m /p /am /mm) statt Freitext im Rufzeichen
   101→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/10
   102→- **Status:** Erledigt
   103→- **Problem:** Operatoren gaben Suffixe wie `/m` (mobil), `/p` (portabel) direkt im Rufzeichen-Feld ein — fehleranfällig und uneinheitlich.
   104→- **Umsetzung:**
   105→  - **Schema-Migration:** Neue Spalte `suffix TEXT DEFAULT NULL` in `signal_reports` (ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN, nicht-destruktiv).
   106→  - **API:** POST/PATCH/GET Reports akzeptieren und liefern `suffix`-Feld. Stats-Participants-Query nutzt `GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT sr.suffix)` als `suffixes`.
   107→  - **UI-Eingabe:** Toggle-Buttons (m, p, am, mm) neben dem Rufzeichen-Feld in LandMode und BundMode. Radio-Verhalten: nur einer aktiv, nochmal klicken = deselect. Blau bei aktiv, grau bei inaktiv.
   108→  - **Auswertung Web:** Teilnehmer-Tabelle zeigt Suffix neben Rufzeichen (z.B. "OE8YML/m").
   109→  - **Auswertung PDF:** Teilnehmer-Liste zeigt Callsign + Suffix.
   110→  - Bestehende Reports ohne Suffix funktionieren weiterhin (NULL = kein Suffix).
   111→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `schema.ts`, `exercises.ts`, `reports.ts`, `pdf.ts` (Routes), `pdf.ts` (Service), `stats.ts`, `LandMode.tsx`, `BundMode.tsx`, `ReportsPage.tsx`, `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`
   112→
   113→### #6 — Rufzeichen doppelt eingeben führt zu Problemen
   114→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/6
   115→- **Status:** Erledigt
   116→- **Problem:** Wenn ein Rufzeichen doppelt eingegeben wird (gleicher Operator + gleicher Repeater), greift die UNIQUE-Constraint der DB und der Server gibt 409 zurück. Der Client versuchte den existierenden Report per `reports.find()` im lokalen State zu finden, um ihn zu PATCHen. Bei veraltetem State passierte **gar nichts** — kein Feedback, kein Update.
   117→- **Fix:** Server liefert bei 409 den existierenden Report als `existing_report` im Response-Body mit. Client nutzt diesen direkt statt im lokalen State zu suchen. `apiFetch` hängt den vollen Response-Body als `error.data` am Error-Objekt an.
   118→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `exercises.ts` (Server), `api.ts`, `LandMode.tsx`, `BundMode.tsx`
   119→

     1→# BOS-ARSA Log Project Memory
     2→
     3→## Environment
     4→- NixOS system — `npm`/`node` not on PATH directly, use `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "..."` to run them
     5→- Monorepo: `/client` (React/Vite) + `/server` (Express/TypeScript)
     6→- SQLite database at `/data/bosarsalog.db`
     7→
     8→## Deployment
     9→- **Always use `./deploy-production.sh`** — deploys via SSH to remote host, builds Docker there
    10→- NEVER use `docker compose up` locally — that's not the production deploy path
    11→
    12→## Key Patterns
    13→- Schema migrations go in `server/src/db/schema.ts` using PRAGMA table_info checks
    14→- Seed data in `server/src/db/seed.ts` — rerun with `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run db:seed"`
    15→- API routes follow RESTful patterns in `server/src/routes/`
    16→- Frontend uses `apiFetch()` wrapper from `client/src/services/api.ts`
    17→- German UI, English code convention
    18→
    19→## Completed Features
    20→- **Login overhaul** (2026-02-15): Replaced callsign+PIN auth with username+password. Two users: `admin`/`BosArsa2024!` and `operator`/`Funk73!ops`. Schema: `admins` table uses `username`/`password_hash`. Role-based access on exercises routes. ExerciseSetupPage: name/start/BL-toggle admin-only.
    21→- **Landesweite restructuring** (2026-02-15): Added `bundesland_code` and `is_custom` columns to repeaters table. Expanded repeater list with comprehensive Austrian OEVSV data (~80 repeaters across OE1-OE9). ExerciseSetupPage restructured with Bundesland selection grid, per-BL repeater sections, custom repeater creation. LandMode tabs grouped by Bundesland with operator callsign badges.
    22→- **GUID exercises + status removal** (2026-02-15): Exercise IDs changed from INTEGER AUTOINCREMENT to TEXT UUIDs (crypto.randomUUID()). Removed exercise status concept entirely (no planned/active/completed). Schema migration detects INTEGER id and recreates all exercise-related tables. All client parseInt() calls removed, exerciseId prop type changed to string.
    23→

     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→set -e
     3→
     4→# Colors for output
     5→RED='\033[0;31m'
     6→GREEN='\033[0;32m'
     7→YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
     8→NC='\033[0m' # No Color
     9→
    10→# Load environment variables
    11→if [ -f ".env.production" ]; then
    12→    source .env.production
    13→else
    14→    echo -e "${RED}Error: .env.production file not found${NC}"
    15→    echo "Copy .env.production.example to .env.production and configure it"
    16→    exit 1
    17→fi
    18→
    19→# Validate required variables
    20→REQUIRED_VARS="DEPLOY_HOST REMOTE_DIR CONTAINER_NAME IMAGE_NAME CONTAINER_PORT SITE_URL JWT_SECRET"
    21→for var in $REQUIRED_VARS; do
    22→    if [ -z "${!var}" ]; then
    23→        echo -e "${RED}Error: $var is not set in .env.production${NC}"
    24→        exit 1
    25→    fi
    26→done
    27→
    28→# Check for --rebuild flag
    29→REBUILD_FLAG=""
    30→if [ "$1" == "--rebuild" ]; then
    31→    REBUILD_FLAG="--no-cache"
    32→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Rebuild flag set — will build without cache${NC}"
    33→fi
    34→
    35→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deploying BOS-ARSA Log to $DEPLOY_HOST ===${NC}"
    36→echo "Host: $DEPLOY_HOST"
    37→echo "Remote dir: $REMOTE_DIR"
    38→echo "Container: $CONTAINER_NAME"
    39→echo "Port: $CONTAINER_PORT"
    40→echo ""
    41→
    42→# Step 1: Sync code to remote host
    43→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 1: Syncing code...${NC}"
    44→GIT_URL=$(git remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null || echo "")
    45→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    46→    if [ -d '$REMOTE_DIR' ]; then
    47→        cd '$REMOTE_DIR' && git pull
    48→    else
    49→        mkdir -p '$(dirname $REMOTE_DIR)'
    50→        git clone '$GIT_URL' '$REMOTE_DIR'
    51→    fi
    52→"
    53→
    54→# Step 2: Copy .env for docker compose
    55→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 2: Copying environment config...${NC}"
    56→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "cat > '$REMOTE_DIR/.env' << 'ENVEOF'
    57→JWT_SECRET=$JWT_SECRET
    58→ENVEOF"
    59→
    60→# Step 3: Build Docker image on remote
    61→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 3: Building Docker image...${NC}"
    62→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    63→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    64→    docker build $REBUILD_FLAG -t '$IMAGE_NAME' .
    65→"
    66→
    67→# Step 4: Restart container (volume is preserved — data persists)
    68→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 4: Restarting container...${NC}"
    69→ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "
    70→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    71→    docker compose up -d --force-recreate --build
    72→"
    73→
    74→# Step 5: Verify deployment
    75→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 5: Verifying deployment...${NC}"
    76→sleep 3
    77→
    78→# Check container status
    79→CONTAINER_STATUS=$(ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "docker ps --filter 'name=$CONTAINER_NAME' --format '{{.Status}}'")
    80→echo "Container status: $CONTAINER_STATUS"
    81→
    82→# Check local port
    83→LOCAL_PORT=$(echo $CONTAINER_PORT | cut -d: -f1)
    84→if ssh "$DEPLOY_HOST" "curl -sf -o /dev/null http://localhost:$LOCAL_PORT/api/v1/auth/login"; then
    85→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Container is running on port $LOCAL_PORT${NC}"
    86→else
    87→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Container not yet responding on port $LOCAL_PORT — may still be starting${NC}"
    88→fi
    89→
    90→# Check public URL
    91→if curl -sf -o /dev/null "$SITE_URL" 2>/dev/null; then
    92→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Site is accessible at $SITE_URL${NC}"
    93→else
    94→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Public URL not yet accessible — configure Cloudflare tunnel${NC}"
    95→fi
    96→
    97→echo ""
    98→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deployment complete ===${NC}"
    99→echo ""
   100→echo "To seed a fresh database:  docker exec $CONTAINER_NAME node dist/server/db/seed.js"
   101→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.

> AGENT

This time the deploy actually rebuilt — you can see `COPY server/` and `COPY client/` were not cached, and `vite build` and `tsc` ran fresh. The previous deploy had all cached layers because the code hadn't been pushed yet.

The issue was that the commits were only local (2 ahead of origin). The deploy script does `git pull` on the remote, so it needs the commits pushed first. Now it's live with the REP input in BundMode.

    97→    return () => {

   327→                />
   328→                <label className="text-xs text-gray-500 hidden sm:inline">REP:</label>
   329→                <select
   330→                  value={blHomeRepeaters[bl.code] || ''}
   331→                  onChange={e => {
   332→                    const val = e.target.value;
   333→                    setBlHomeRepeaters(prev => ({ ...prev, [bl.code]: val }));
   334→                    saveHomeRepeater(bl.code, val);
   335→                  }}
   336→                  className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24 sm:w-32 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   337→                >
   338→                  <option value="">—</option>
   339→                  {linkedRepeaters.filter(r => r.bundesland_code === bl.code).map(r => (
   340→                    <option key={r.id} value={r.short_name}>{r.site_name || r.short_name}{r.band ? ` ${r.band}` : ''}</option>
   341→                  ))}
   342→                  {linkedRepeaters.filter(r => r.bundesland_code !== bl.code).length > 0 && (
   343→                    <option disabled>──────</option>
   344→                  )}
   345→                  {linkedRepeaters.filter(r => r.bundesland_code !== bl.code).map(r => (
   346→                    <option key={r.id} value={r.short_name}>{r.site_name || r.short_name}{r.band ? ` ${r.band}` : ''}</option>
   347→                  ))}
   348→                </select>
   349→              </div>
   350→            </div>
   351→
   352→            {!isCollapsed && (
   353→              <div>
   354→                {addRepBl === bl.code && (
   355→                  <div className="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-50 border-b flex items-center gap-1 flex-wrap">
   356→                    <input
   357→                      type="text"
   358→                      value={newRepName}
   359→                      onChange={e => setNewRepName(e.target.value)}
   360→                      placeholder="Name"
   361→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-28 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   362→                      autoFocus
   363→                    />
   364→                    <input
   365→                      type="text"
   366→                      value={newRepFreq}
   367→                      onChange={e => setNewRepFreq(e.target.value)}
   368→                      placeholder="MHz"
   369→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   370→                    />
   371→                    <input
   372→                      type="text"
   373→                      value={newRepCallsign}
   374→                      onChange={e => setNewRepCallsign(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   375→                      placeholder="Rufz."
   376→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   377→                    />
   378→                    <button
   379→                      onClick={() => addRepeater(bl.code)}
   380→                      className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs"
   381→                    >
   382→                      OK
   383→                    </button>
   384→                    <button
   385→                      onClick={() => { setAddRepBl(null); setNewRepName(''); setNewRepFreq(''); setNewRepCallsign(''); }}
   386→                      className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-xs"
   387→                    >
   388→                      Abbrechen
   389→                    </button>
   390→                  </div>
   391→                )}
   392→                <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   393→                  {blBezirke.map(bz => {
   394→                    const bzReports = blReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   395→                    return (
   396→                      <BezirkRow
   397→                        key={bz.code}
   398→                        bezirk={bz}
   399→                        reports={bzReports}
   400→                        availableBezirke={blBezirke}
   401→                        linkedRepeaters={linkedRepeaters}
   402→                        defaultRepeaterId={defaultRepId}
   403→                        onSubmit={(repeaterId, form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, repeaterId, form)}
   404→                        onEdit={handleEdit}
   405→                        onDelete={handleDelete}
   406→                        onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   407→                        editingId={editingId}
   408→                        editForm={editForm}
   409→                        onEditChange={setEditForm}
   410→                        onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   411→                        onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   412→                        dragReportId={dragReportId}
   413→                        onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   414→                        onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   415→                      />
   416→                    );
   417→                  })}
   418→                  {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk + entry form */}
   419→                  {(() => {
   420→                    const bzCodes = new Set(blBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   421→                    const unassigned = blReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   422→                    return (
   423→                      <BezirkRow
   424→                        bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}
   425→                        reports={unassigned}
   426→                        availableBezirke={blBezirke}
   427→                        linkedRepeaters={linkedRepeaters}
   428→                        defaultRepeaterId={defaultRepId}
   429→                        onSubmit={(repeaterId, form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', repeaterId, form)}
   430→                        onEdit={handleEdit}
   431→                        onDelete={handleDelete}
   432→                        onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   433→                        editingId={editingId}
   434→                        editForm={editForm}
   435→                        onEditChange={setEditForm}
   436→                        onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   437→                        onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   438→                        dragReportId={dragReportId}
   439→                        onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   440→                        onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   441→                      />
   442→                    );
   443→                  })()}
   444→                </div>
   445→              </div>
   446→            )}
   447→          </div>
   448→        );
   449→      })}
   450→    </div>
   451→  );
   452→}
   453→
   454→interface BezirkRowProps {
   455→  bezirk: any;
   456→  reports: any[];
   457→  availableBezirke: any[];
   458→  linkedRepeaters: any[];
   459→  defaultRepeaterId: number;
   460→  onSubmit: (repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => void;
   461→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   462→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   463→  onMoveToBezirk: (reportId: number, bezirkCode: string | null) => void;
   464→  editingId: number | null;
   465→  editForm: EntryForm;
   466→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   467→  onEditSave: () => void;
   468→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   469→  dragReportId: number | null;
   470→  onDragStart: (reportId: number) => void;
   471→  onDragEnd: () => void;
   472→}
   473→
   474→function BezirkRow({ bezirk, reports, availableBezirke, linkedRepeaters, defaultRepeaterId, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, onMoveToBezirk, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel, dragReportId, onDragStart, onDragEnd }: BezirkRowProps) {
   475→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
   476→  const [repeaterId, setRepeaterId] = useState(defaultRepeaterId);
   477→  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
   478→  const callsignRef = useRef<CallsignInputRef>(null);
   479→  const rapportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   480→
   481→  useEffect(() => {
   482→    if (defaultRepeaterId && !repeaterId) setRepeaterId(defaultRepeaterId);
   483→  }, [defaultRepeaterId]);
   484→
   485→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   486→    e.preventDefault();
   487→    if (!form.callsign && !form.operator) return;
   488→    if (!repeaterId) return;
   489→    onSubmit(repeaterId, form);
   490→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
   491→    setTimeout(() => callsignRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   492→  };
   493→
   494→  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
   495→    e.preventDefault();
   496→    setDragOver(false);
   497→    if (dragReportId) {
   498→      const targetCode = bezirk.code === '??' ? null : bezirk.code;
   499→      onMoveToBezirk(dragReportId, targetCode);
   500→    }
   501→  };
   502→
   503→  const saveOperatorField = (field: 'name' | 'qth', value: string) => {
   504→    if (!form.operator?.id) return;
   505→    const original = field === 'name' ? (form.operator.name || '') : (form.operator.qth || '');
   506→    if (value === original) return;
   507→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${form.operator.id}`, {
   508→      method: 'PATCH',
   509→      body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value || null }),
   510→    }).then(() => {
   511→      setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: { ...f.operator, [field]: value || null } }));
   512→    }).catch(() => {});
   513→  };
   514→
   515→  return (
   516→    <div
   517→      className={`px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors ${dragOver && dragReportId ? 'bg-blue-100 ring-2 ring-blue-400 ring-inset' : ''}`}
   518→      onDragOver={e => { if (dragReportId) { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); } }}
   519→      onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
   520→      onDrop={handleDrop}
   521→    >
   522→      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 mb-1">
   523→        <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bezirk.code === '??' ? 'bg-[#ffc107] text-gray-800' : bezirk.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   524→          {bezirk.code === '??' ? '??' : bezirk.code}
   525→        </span>
   526→        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{bezirk.name}</span>
   527→        {reports.length > 0 && <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0">({reports.length})</span>}
   528→      </div>
   529→
   530→      {reports.length > 0 && (
   531→        <table className="w-full text-xs mb-1">
   532→          <tbody>
   533→            {reports.map((r, idx) => (
   534→              <tr
   535→                key={r.id}
   536→                className={`hover:bg-blue-50 group ${dragReportId === r.id ? 'opacity-40' : ''}`}
   537→                draggable={editingId !== r.id}
   538→                onDragStart={e => { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; onDragStart(r.id); }}
   539→                onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
   540→              >
   541→                {editingId === r.id ? (
   542→                  <td colSpan={6} className="py-0.5">
   543→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 rounded p-1 flex-wrap" onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); onEditSave(); } }}>
   544→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   545→                      <input value={editForm.opName} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 hidden sm:block" />
   546→                      <input value={editForm.opQth} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opQth: e.target.value })} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16 hidden sm:block" />
   547→                      <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" autoFocus />
   548→                      <input value={editForm.notes} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, notes: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sonst." className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" />
   549→                      <select
   550→                        value={editForm.bezirkCode}
   551→                        onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, bezirkCode: e.target.value })}
   552→                        className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16"
   553→                        title="Bezirk"
   554→                      >
   555→                        <option value="">??</option>
   556→                        {availableBezirke.map(bz => <option key={bz.code} value={bz.code}>{bz.code}</option>)}
   557→                      </select>
   558→                      <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   559→                      <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   560→                    </div>
   561→                  </td>
   562→                ) : (
   563→                  <>
   564→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-400 w-4 cursor-grab">{idx + 1}</td>
   565→                    <td className="py-0.5 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   566→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}{r.suffix || ''}</span>
   567→                      {r.operator_name && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs">{r.operator_name}</span>}
   568→                      {r.operator_qth && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400 text-xs hidden sm:inline">{r.operator_qth}</span>}
   569→                    </td>
   570→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-blue-600 text-xs cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.repeater_name || ''}</td>
   571→                    <td className="py-0.5 font-mono text-gray-500 w-16 sm:w-24 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   572→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}
   573→                    </td>
   574→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-500 cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.notes || ''}</td>
   575→                    <td className="py-0.5 w-4">
   576→                      <button onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">x</button>
   577→                    </td>
   578→                  </>
   579→                )}
   580→              </tr>
   581→            ))}
   582→          </tbody>
   583→        </table>
   584→      )}
   585→
   586→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   587→        {linkedRepeaters.length > 0 && (
   588→          <select
   589→            value={repeaterId || ''}
   590→            onChange={e => setRepeaterId(parseInt(e.target.value))}
   591→            className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs max-w-[80px] sm:max-w-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   592→          >
   593→            {linkedRepeaters.map(r => (
   594→              <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>
   595→                {r.site_name || r.short_name}{r.callsign ? ` (${r.callsign})` : ''}
   596→              </option>
   597→            ))}
   598→          </select>
   599→        )}
   600→        <CallsignInput
   601→          ref={callsignRef}
   602→          value={form.callsign}
   603→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v, operator: null, opName: '', opQth: '' }))}
   604→          onSelect={op => {
   605→            setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign, opName: op.name || '', opQth: op.qth || '' }));
   606→            setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50);
   607→          }}
   608→          onEnter={() => setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50)}
   609→          className="w-24 sm:w-28"
   610→        />
   611→        <div className="flex gap-0.5">
   612→          {SUFFIXES.map(s => (
   613→            <button
   614→              key={s}
   615→              type="button"
   616→              onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, suffix: f.suffix === s ? '' : s }))}
   617→              className={`px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono border ${form.suffix === s ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200'}`}
   618→              title={s}
   619→            >
   620→              {s.slice(1)}
   621→            </button>
   622→          ))}
   623→        </div>
   624→        <input
   625→          type="text"
   626→          value={form.opName}
   627→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opName: e.target.value }))}
   628→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('name', e.target.value)}
   629→          placeholder="Name"
   630→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   631→        />
   632→        <input
   633→          type="text"
   634→          value={form.opQth}
   635→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opQth: e.target.value }))}
   636→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('qth', e.target.value)}
   637→          placeholder="QTH"
   638→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   639→        />
   640→        <input
   641→          ref={rapportRef}
   642→          type="text"
   643→          value={form.rapport}
   644→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   645→          placeholder="5/9"
   646→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   647→          onKeyDown={e => {
   648→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   649→              e.preventDefault();
   650→              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));
   651→            }
   652→          }}
   653→        />
   654→        <input
   655→          type="text"
   656→          value={form.notes}
   657→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}
   658→          placeholder="Sonst."
   659→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs flex-1 min-w-[40px] sm:min-w-[60px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   660→        />
   661→        <button type="submit" title="Rapport speichern (Enter)" className="bg-[#c8102e] hover:bg-[#a00d24] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs flex-shrink-0">
   662→          + ↵
   663→        </button>
   664→      </form>
   665→    </div>
   666→  );
   667→}

    47→---
    48→
    49→## Offene Issues
    50→
    51→### #3 — Eigener Repeater pro Operator → verschoben nach BundMode
    52→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/3
    53→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    54→- **Änderung:** home_repeater ist jetzt ein **per-Übung/per-BL-Feld** im BundMode, nicht mehr ein Operator-Stammdaten-Attribut.
    55→- **Revert (Stammdaten/Auswertung):**
    56→  - `home_repeater` aus POST/PATCH Operators entfernt (Spalte in DB bleibt für Kompatibilität).
    57→  - Participants-Queries in exercises.ts, reports.ts, pdf.ts: `o.home_repeater` entfernt.
    58→  - Participant-Interface (client + server): `home_repeater` entfernt.
    59→  - OperatorsPage: Repeater-Spalte entfernt (Tabelle, Add-Form, Edit-Modus).
    60→  - ReportsPage + AggregatedReportsPage: Repeater-Spalte aus Teilnehmer-Tabelle entfernt.
    61→  - PDF: REP.-Spalte aus Teilnehmer-Liste entfernt, Name-Spalte wieder breiter.
    62→- **Neue Umsetzung (BundMode):**
    63→  - **Schema-Migration:** Neue Spalte `home_repeater TEXT DEFAULT NULL` in `exercise_repeaters`-Tabelle.
    64→  - **API:** POST/PATCH `/:id/repeaters/:rid` akzeptieren `home_repeater`. PATCH unterstützt partielle Updates (operator_callsign und/oder home_repeater).
    65→  - **Socket:** Neues Event `home_repeater_updated` — Server broadcastet an Exercise-Room.
    66→  - **BundMode UI:** REP-Input neben OP-Input im BL-Header. Speichert on blur (POST-dann-PATCH Pattern wie OP). Socket-Sync zwischen Clients.
    67→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `schema.ts`, `operators.ts`, `exercises.ts`, `reports.ts`, `pdf.ts` (Routes), `pdf.ts` (Service), `socket.ts`, `stats.ts`, `OperatorsPage.tsx`, `ReportsPage.tsx`, `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`, `BundMode.tsx`
    68→
    69→### #5 — Eventuell mehrere OPs bei Simplex
    70→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/5
    71→- **Status:** Geschlossen — bereits möglich
    72→- Mehrere OPs können kommagetrennt im OP-Feld eingetragen werden, z.B. `OE1OP1, OE2OP2`. Das Feld ist ein Freitext-Input ohne Beschränkung auf ein einzelnes Rufzeichen.
    73→
    74→### #8 — Download Auswertung komplett — Server-seitiges PDF
    75→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/8
    76→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed

> AGENT

Done. The edit-mode Bezirk dropdown in BundMode now shows **all Bundesländer** with their Bezirke, grouped by `<optgroup>` (e.g. "OE1 Wien", "OE5 Salzburg", "OE2 Kärnten"). This lets you move a callsign from any BL section to any other BL directly.

Changes:
- **BundMode.tsx**: `BezirkRow` now receives `allBezirke` and `allBundeslaender` props. The edit dropdown uses `<optgroup>` per Bundesland showing all Bezirke with `code + name`.
- **CHANGELOG** updated with the new Bezirk dropdown behavior and the REP dropdown details.

     5→### #1 — Rapport soll verschiebbar innerhalb der Bezirke sein
